~Bansan me yasa a d



*Alhamdulillah nasake dawowa, hallau da sabon buk me cike da tsantsar yaudara cin amana, zalunci da fatan zaku biyo yar mutan bauchi labarin da zai kasance a cikin garin Bauchin Yakub nafi jin daɗin rubuta labari a cikin jahata, garina duniyata Long life bauchi*👍

*Page.....1/2*

A garin abuja tafe yake cikin shida na hanun damarsa, Adam Tahir Waziri suke tafi gyara rike briefcase ɗinshi yayi cikin nutsuwa ya karasa jikin motarsu, ya karewa Abokinshi kallo girgiza kai, yayi cikin damuwa yace.
"Sadauki, Barde ya aikoni gurinka." Murmushin karfin hali yayi sannan yace.
"AMMA kasan bai dace muyi magana anan ba ko tunda kaga kowa yafito, don Allah mushiga mota sai ka fada min abinda ya, aiko ka dashi." A sanyaye ya kai maganar kana ganinshi, kasan sanyin halinshi yayi, yawa amma idan ranshi ya baci babu me iya tankarashi sai Kakanshi wanda ya, haifi mahaifiyarshi wato Baba ladan(Ladannin bauchi kenan) cikaken malami ne, wanda yasan me yakeyi, sam baya cikin malaman marasa mutunci dan Baba ladan datijon kwaraine, shine kadai wanda yake iya tankwara Sadauki ko yana so ko baya so sai, yayi abinda aka sashi, ko aka hana shi.

     Bude motar Adam yayi, shima sadauki ya zaga, yashiga motar a hankali adam yake jan motar, hankalinshi nakan hanya gyaran murya yayi. Sannan ya saci kallon Abokinshi tun nayaranta, yanda ya shiga damuwa a cikin shekara biyu jal, sannan ya cigaba da tuki sauke numfashi yayi kafin yace.

" Sadauki, Ina son kafaɗa min meke damun rayuwartane, shin ko kana bakin cikin koranka da NIA sukayi ne, ni dai bansan meke damunka ba, amma koma meye yaci ace ka mallaki iyalinka, babu daɗi magidanci kamanka, iyayenka suna jin zafin rashin auren da bakayi ba, bazan manta shekaru biyu, zuwa uku kace zaka gabarta mana da Yarinyar da zaka aura, abin damuwa kafin wannan lokacin kome ya lalace har yau ban sake jin kayi min zance wata yarinya, Shin meke faruwane anya kana cikin jerin maza kuwa, dan barde yace na kaika asibiti a dubaka, ko kai," sai yayi shiru yana kallon Sadauki, wanda ya lumshe idanunshi jin yace kokai ɗin  yasa ya buɗe idanun shi tarr akan At waxiri, tsuke fuska yayi wacce takarawa fuskarsa kyau da haiba, tabon sallarshi tasake fitowa sosai sajenshi da kasumbar da take, fuskarshi tasake fitowa sosai, dogon hancinsa wacce ta dace da fuskarsa tayi zirr, sai bakinsa me kyau ja da ita, gashin baki haɗi da, yar gemu ce tasake kawata fuskarshi bafulataninsa sak yafito, bankawa A.t waziri harara yayi cike da ɓacin rai yace.
"Kutt, ni ɗin ne ba, namiji ba sannan akaini asibiti dan a duba ni ko, Wallahi xanci ubanka kuma sai na faɗawa Hakimi abinda kuke min da Baba ladan kun maidani mace, akan me zaku dame ni,da maganar Y'a mace duk shaiɗanune su, Wallahi aka sake turo ka, sai na harbeka dan wallahi babu abinda ya dame ɗan iska mayen mata kawai sauke ni anan na koma gida akafa dan banza me shegen fitina idan nasake ganinka a gidanmu sai naci ubanka.."


        Dariya A.T yake har yana, buga sitiyerin motar dakyar ya tsayi da dariya yace.
"Muje gida Ubangiji kasa barde na gida, sai yau zanyi kallo san raina,"  Sadauki bai sake kula shi ba dan ya lura A.T waziri baida mutunci banda ɗan uwanshine kuma abokinshi, Uwa uba sirikinshine, dan yana Auren Nazifa kanwar Sadauki da yaci ubanshi la,ada waje,

         haka suka isa, gida dan munafurci har ciki A.t waziri yabi Sadauki ganin, motar jami'an tsoron gidan yasa yayi dariya ƴace.
"Alhamdulillah, Senator President yana gida muje na gaida sirikina."


      A sanyaye Sadauki, ya shiga cikin gidan dan yasan karyarsa takare kuma wa'adin da aka ɗiba masa duk ya kare, a hankali ya tura kofar yashiga, hango mahaifiyarshi yayi da kannensa suna, hira.
"Assalamun Alaikumun." Shiga falon yayi tare da sallama,


"Amin Waalaikumun Salam."
     Dake sun ɗauki muryanshii mikewa kannenshi sukayi tare da gaishe shi, ɗaga musu hannun yayi, sunsan da haka karɓan jakarshi ɗaya dagq cikin kannenshi tayi takai ɗakinshi dake can gefen part ɗinsu, amma ta cikin gidansu ake shiga akwai wani hanya waje, 
            zama yayi a cikin kujerun falon, haka ma A.t waziri, cikin kunya ya gaida sirikarsa murmushi tayi sannan tace.
"Ina angona da kishiyata, suke yaushe zaka kawo minsune,"


   yar dariya yayi, sannan yace.
"Kardai  Nazifa bata faɗa miki, bai da lafiya, wai hakori yake shi ys basu zo ba."

"Ayya ashh, Allah ya bashi lafiya, insha Allah zamu zo duba shi." tace.
Sunkuyar da kanshi Adam yayi, cike da jinkunya yace.
"Ummi ai da kinhuta, abinki tunda jikin da sauki sosai."


      Murmushi tayi, sannan tace.
"Karka damu, zamu zo ma, "

        kallon Sadauki tayi, wanda tun shigowarsu ya sunkuyar da kanshi, tace.
"Sadeeq lafiya? kuwa ka sunkuyar da kanka,."

      Ɗ'ago kanshi yayi, cikin nutsuwa , haɗi da sauke ajiyar zuciya cikin sanyin murya yace.
"Ummi barde yana, nan ne"

"Eh ina nan, bakayi tsamanin ina gida ba, ko hmm, Kaje giɗansu Lateefa," wani farin datijo, wanda bazai haura 72 ba yace masa,

        ɗago kai sadauki yayi cike da fargaba yace.
"Eh naje"
"Toh ya kukayi da ita ?," yasake jefa masa tambaya.

Murmushi yayi dan yasan ynz zai lalata duk wani shirin da mahaifinsa yake akanshi,yace.
"Naje itama kaman sauran ta cika rawankai da son sanin ita ɗin, yar wani ce."

  "Kenan duk, yan matan da nake nima maka, kowacce tana da matsala kenan, "

"Eh mana, barde katura ni, gidansu Hasina, naje yarinyar ta cika surutu, naje gidansu Sadeeqa, ita kuma bata da bam banci da wawayen yaran, kace naje gidansu Fatima, ita kuma haɗa samaruka, naje gidansu Afnan gwada min, iko tayi dan babanta jinin sarautar kano ne, ga gidan lateefa, don Allah kuraba ni da maganar matan nan" yace.
           Shiru Alhaji Ahmad Aliyu Galadima yayi, cike da al'ajabi jinjina kai yayi,

*Page 7/8*

"Don  Allah hajiya kaka karki cemin babu kowa ya gujemu abinda zamuci ma yafi karfinmu Hajiya, kitaimaka ko wankinkine zanyi wallahi. "

Karshe maganar tayi cikin kuka,

           Tausayine ya rufe Hajiya kaka, dan Safeenat bata san da mutane  a ɗakin ba,  duk sai ta basu tausayi Hajiya Abida ce mahaifiyar sadauki sai Aunty Adama, sai Aunty Baraka itace karama dan Baba lada, Yaranshi biyar ne, Mahaifiyar Adama Hajiya Amina itace babba sai Hajiya Abida, Aunty Adama sai Halima, da Aunty baraka duk suna auren manya ne, dan Maman Sadauki ita da Maman Adam suke abuja.

          Duk sai tausayin Safeena ya hanasu magana can hajiya kaka ta miko mata kular abinci sannan tace.
"Ci abinci Nanna zan ɗaukeki aiki kuma, Insha Allah zan biyaki kuɗi yanda zaki kula da mahaifiyarki su kuma Ubangiji ya ganardasu ci abinci."

           Girgiza kai Nanna tayi hawaye na zuba daga idanunta cikin dishashiyar murya tace.
"Hajiya kaka, bazan iya cin kome ba tunda Mama ma bataci ba kawai zan juye a laidane dan matukar suka ga kularki zasu haɗani dasu yaya, abugeni karshe mama bazata sake same abincin ba."

        Miqa mata laida, Aunty Adama tayi. Juye abincin tayi jikinta na rawa tafita ko sallama bata musu ba, sai da takai bakin kofa tace.
"Hajiya kaka zan shigo anjima bari na kaiwa Mama abinci taci tana jin yunwa."

        Haka tafita bayan ta kunshe abincin A cikin himar ɗinta.
.....
Bayan fitarta Hajiya Abida ta kalle mahaifiyarta a raunane tace.
"Kamar yar maman Anas ko"

        gyaɗa kai Hajiya kaka tayi cikin damuwa sannan tace.
"Ai baiwar Allah nan tana ganin, Jarabawa duk yaranta sun juya mata baya kamar ba ita bace tayi faɗi tashin ganin sunyi karatu ba, har kosai da masa tayi yau an wayi gari yaran sun gujeta har gwara mahaifinsu yana taimaka mata amma banda yaranta sai dai su taimakawa Fatu,"

                     Jikinsune yayi masifar sanyi kaman zasuyi kuka haka sukayita tattaunawa karshe aka tsayi da magana akan Nanna zata rinkawa Hajiya kaka wanke wanke, da shara girki akwai meyi, duk wata zasu na biyarta dubu huɗu sannan maman Sadauki tacire dubu biyar tabawa kaka abawa Nanna takaiwa mamanta Aunty Adama ɗubu biyu haka ma Aunty baraka dubu biyu, suka haɗa mata dubu tara hajiya kaka takarq dubu uku, sabida karatun safeenat.
.....

    Nannah kuwa tana isa gida ɗakinsu ta wucce tasamu mama na kwance, ɗauko kwano tayi ta juye abincin sannan ta tashi mamanta tace.
"Mahma tashi kici abinci, kar ya wucce da zafi zai fi miki daɗi."

           Tashi tayi ta zauna sannan, nannah ta mika mata cokali da shinkafa tafara ci a hankali tana kallon Nannah, yanda ta zuba mata ido tana zubda kwalla masu zafi, batayi aune ba taji an rufeta da duka nafitan hankali da gudu ta rarrafa gurin Mamanta aka fincikota ana kwallo da ita, cikin kakkausar murya Anas yake cewa.
"Dan kaniyarki babanmu zaki zubdawa mutunci, duk yanda yake kokarin kyautata mana, amma sai kinje waje niman abinci kwaɗayayir banza kawai ai baki da laifi Mamace ta barki kiyi haka,"

        bai fasa dukarta ba, hajiya fatu kuwa dariya take sosai dan ita ta haɗa bom ɗin,

      Maman Anas ganin zai kashe mata yarta yasa ta dafa bango tazo har inda yake tasaka hanun ya kwaci Nannah wacce ko ihu bata iyawa, kallonshi mama tayi cikin murmushi me ɗauke da tsantsar kulawa tace.
"Anas kenan tayi abinda ka gazaƴi kai da yan uwanka shine zaka kashe min ita, Allah ya shiryeku baki ɗaya. Amma daga yau karka sake dukar min y'a idan kuma kasake, tabbas ubangiji zai bi mata hakkinta ina bakin cikin ako yaushe zan iya tafiya na barta cikin mugayen mutane amma idan na duba, sai naga ina cikin farin ciki ko ba kome tana da yan uwa masu kula da ita," tunda tafaɗi haka ta koma ta zauna sai hakki take hawayen da take boyewa suka kuwa ɓalle kaman da bakin kwarya, jikinshine yayi sanyi yafita a ɗakin ya rasa me yake damunshi ya watsar da alamarin mahaifiyarshi tunda ya fara aikin koyarwa, hankalinshi ya bar kan mahaifiyartasa. Da wannan tunanin yabar gidan baki ɗaya.

       Miqa mata abinci Mama tayi, tana kallonta kukan da take boyewane ya kwace mata dukkansu biyu kuka suke, sosai abincin da basu ciba kenan dan Safeenat duk halin da take matukar taga mahaifiyarta a ciki koda abincine takan bar mahaifiyarta taci takoshi kafin ita taci a haka suke rayuwarsu.

          ~~~~
   Washi gari....
Bayan an karya wanke wanke gidansu Nanna tayi, sannan ta shiga ɗakinsu tana kallon Mama da take zaune akan abin sallah tace.
"Mama zan shiga gidan hajiya kaka, ko Allah zai sa musami abin karyawa,kuma ban faɗa miki bane daman na tambayi hajiya kaka ko zata yarda nakama mata aikin gidan ko nawane ana bani nasamu kuɗin Makaranta da, Kuɗin Maganinki."

     Cike da tsoron take kallon Mamanta tana gudu karta ce a'a sai taga tayi murmushi tace.
"Yar albarka, kije Ubangiji yabaki sa'a yasa hanyar farin cikinki kenan,Idan ban mutu ba Safeenatu zanyi kallo dan Nayi  imani da Allah zaki share min hawayena, Insha Allah sai kin zama zakarar gajin dafi."

   "Amin mamana, ni kaɗai bari naje na dawo." tace sannan tafita.

........
Da sallama tashiga gidan a falon farko tasame hajiya kaka, zama tayi ta gaida Hajiya kanta a sunkuye, haɗa tea hajiya kaka tayi ta mika mata karɓa tayi tace. " Nagode Hajiya kaka bazan sha ba, zan kaiwa Mamana dan tafine so."

        Cike da jin daɗi hajiya kaka tace.
"Toh kin kyauta, maganar aiki kuwa Mamanki na abuja ta ɗaukeki zakina min wanke wanke da shara bawani kaya bane akwai Maman baraka tana girki ai kinsanta tunda matar yahuzane, duk wata za,ana biyanki dubu huɗu, zakiyi wankewanke da safe sai kuma yamma kisake shara kuwa, sau biyune, ga wannan kicire kuɗin makarantarki da unifoam da kayan makarantar saura,kinkawa Mamanki kinko yanzu kifara aikin."

        Kuka take abin tausayi yarinya yar kimanin shekara goma sha biyu, ita kw kokarin kulawa da mahaifiyarta, dakyar tayi aiki bayan ta gama tazo har ɗakin hajiya kaka tana, zuba mata godiya haɗi dakuka bayan ta gama hajiya kaka tace.

"Idan rana tayi kizo ki amshi abincin rana da dare ma, haka Allah yayi maki albarka."

      "Amin mungode." mikewa tayi tabar gidan cike da murna, tana shiga takaiwa mama kuɗin tayi mata bayani, ware dubu biyar mama tayi ta bata tac,   taje makarantarsu tabiya, jikinta na rawa tatafi, cikin ikon Allah ta haɗu da Abdulkadeer, shi dakansa yaje ya mata kome, sannan suka dawo gida.

         ~~~
Abinda mutane gidan basu sani bakenan, ganinta suke da safe tafita bayan tagama musu wanke wanke, taje tayi na gidan hajiya kaka, abin ya basu haushi dan haka suka kai saranta ga me kuɗin gero, dake Allah ya taimaka shi bazai baka kuɗinshi ba amma zayi iya kokarinshi na ka tsaya da kafarka gwalesu yayi inda yace.
"Idan tayi wanke wanke da shara ai dan ta taimaki kanta da uwarta ce, kuma ni banga aibinta ba, sai ma birgeni da tayi dan ina son mutum me kokarin tsayawa da kafarshi ba irin yaranku ba, da karatun bokon bata damesu ba sai shegen janyowa mutum abin faɗa, babu wanda ya taɓa tsareni sabida safeena ko Anas da Abdukadeer amma kullum sai an tsayidani sabida shaiɗanun yayanku."

           Haka yayi musu tass sannan suka fita daga harka safeenat.


*Aunty Safeenat tace min All d Best*👍

*page 5/6*

Tun daga kofar gidan take zubda k'walla  cike  da bakin cikin abinda ake mata babu yanda  ta iya tunda  ba y'au suka fara ba, kuma sunsan  sarai  har  Manya sun shigo  maganar tunda an bada ita, amma tana tsaka Au kwace  mata mijin da zata aura me yasa ake mana hakane, muryarta  a raunane  tashiga gidan. So take ta boye,  karayar  fa tarike zuciyarta amma ta gaza  kaman saman ita Suke jira,  duk suka fashe  da dariya suna kallonta hajiya Fatu itace ta fara magana da cewa. 

"Wallahi  idan har ina gidan nan,  Nanna  keda samun  miji  sai na aurar  da yarana mata,  duk suka shige d'akin mijinsu  subarki Ke dai nagadama kiyi aure, kaman yanda Uwanki tayi  mana bakin cikin haihuwar  maza haka kema,  zamu hanaki  aure sai  kin aurar fa yaranmu,,,,,,,, Juyawa  tayi da fuska tana kallon abokiyar Zamanta tacigaba  da cewa. 
"Ko ba haka ba,  Zuwaira."

        Ita Wacce aka kira da zuwaira wani yatsina fuska tayi kafin  tace. 
"Eh mana,  hegiya sai kyau kaman ita tayi kanta Ai sai autata tayi aure,  sannan Mu had'a y'ar banza da d'an tasha,  ya durje mana ita son ranmu, as I haihuwar irin wayanan arzikine a gurinmu idan da y'ata ce sai governor amma dake y'ar kishiya ce, wallahi ko akaina,  kuma dole tayi min aikin gidana, "

         dariya  y'an matan gidan suka saka,  a hankali  tashige d'akinta wanda ya na mahaifiyarta ce, bayan  rasuwarta  takoma d'akin da zama,  kuka take sosai dak'yar ta tashi tana cire kaya sannan  tanema wani tasaka bata  gama nine kayan ba, aka bankade labule d'akin kerere ta tsaya  mata,  akai  cike da rashin kunya da futsira tace.

"Nanna kifito wanke wanke najiranki k'insan bayi zamuyi ba,  tunda my baby bakinki bane "

      ba sabon  Abu bane, a gurinta murmushi tayi irin Wanda ake bawa Wanda ya nufeka fa sharri haushi tace. 
"Karki damu kinsan dan wanke wanken gidanan ba wani yawa ne dasu ba, zanyi kuma idan naso zan iya d'aukar me aiki tayi min, bani da raayin hakane shi yasa nakeyi da kaina kije zanzo, "

         Fuuuu Mimi tafita, kichin taje gurin mahaifiyata hajiya Fati tace. 
"Hajiya naje kiranta amma tazageni har dake Wai mune, matsiyata idan tagadama sai ta dauki me aiki bad dameta ba, ba tunda tana yawon bin maza dan haka naje sai tayi niyya zatayi. "


"Kutt lallai wuyar safeena ya isa yanka Ke Safeena " Hajiya fatu tasheka mata kira.

          Cikin sanyin jiki tafito ta rakube jikin kofar kichin d'in tace. 
"Hajiya gani nan ina gyara dakine."

           Shareta hajiya fatu tayi tacigaba da talgenta kaman babu wani mugun nufi a ranta, tana gamawa tafito daga kichin d'in kafin Nanna ta ankara ta rufeta fa duka, 😭😭😭 Tana yi tana zaginta tare da jifarta fa mugun nufi. 

          Sai da tamata likiss sannan tace. 
"Maza tashi ki min wanke wankena yar iska,"


         *Wacece Safeenat Haliru me kud'in gero*

Alhaji Haliru me kud'in gero,babban d'an kasuwa ne a nan bauchi yana da manyan shaguna a kasuwanin bauchi fa kuma wasu unguwanin,  gaskiya  Allah ya buda masa sosai amma kuma,  mugun matsolone,  dan ana sake masa titular Shinkafa, Gero, Masara, Dawa,  Aca,Aya,  wake, waken soya, yana  sai da kayanshi kasa da furashin kasuwa, amma kuma gidanshi bazaka  taba  ganin anci Shinkafa ba sai ko haihuwa ko biki,  Alhaji haliru yana da mata uku,  Ada amma uwar gidan ta rasu Itace maman Anas,  Itace sukayi Auren saurayi fa budurwa,  ya aurenta tree ana yaron shagon Babanta,  ganin amanarshi  babanta ya bashi aurenta. 

          Maman Anas Wacce sunanta, Saratu tana da matukar kirki fa hankali da nutsuwa, kuma cikin Ikon Allah da akayi auren Ubangiji Ya basu haihuwa Anas shine farko sai Abdulkadeer, sai Nura bata sake haihuwa ba sai da tayi shekara goma, 

      dan lokacin Alhaji haliru ya auri hajiya fatu,  tana shiga ta haife Imam, sunan Babanta ne,  IBRAHIM,  sai kuma tayi ta wabi idan tahaihu sai yaran Sukoma, har Suka sake samun ciki atare da Maman Anas, 

       zaman dasuke tsakanin Maman Anas,  da hajiya fatu zaman hakuri ne,  dan Tana matukar Cutar da Mama Anas amma dake Tana da hakuri  babu wanda ya halkanta da haka.

               Gashi Allah ya budawa haliru lokacin sosai,  cikin Ikon Allah cikinsu  ya tsofa,  har suka haihu Hajiya fatu ta haifi Mace fa kwana biyu sannan  Maman  Anas ta haihu  inda tasame y'arta kyakyawan, nan y'an uwa da dangi suka rufawa,  Maman Anas Dan Itace ta dade bata haihu ba,  abinda yayi  masifar bata mata rai ganin yanda kowa yake rawan kafa akan Maman Anas, 

          Bayan suna ta tsiro dawani mugun hali dan ta cusgunawa Maman Anas inda ta juya hankalin me gidan,  ki inda Maman Anas  take baya kalla,  Abu wasa wasa, har suka yaye Au Safeena dake sunan Mahaifiyar Haliru ce suna kiranta da Nanna,  ita kuma Y'ar hajiya Fatu,  aka samata  Sailuba sunan da Uwarta takeso kenan, 

         Tun daga nan Sailuba, haihuwa ta tsayuwa Hajiya fatu,  akai akai, kuma duk kunika sai da tayi  haihuwa biyar duk mata, Imam ne kawai na miji, Bayan Sailuba ta haifi Fauziya sai Faiza, Sai lawiza,  sai  Mimi me sunanta suna kiranta da Mimi, Tana goyon  Fauziya  ya auro Zuwaira bazawara ce,  inda tazo da Rukayya, Agolarta ana shiga tasame ciki inda suka haihu a jere da Hajiya fatu ita Faiza.  Itakuma Ta haifi Bilkisa, sai Hasiya, sai Asma'u sai y'ar karamar ta, Safiya.  Babban abin takaicin agidan shine yaran gidan basu son karatu idan kacire, yaran Maman Anas, suma  Itace take d'aukar nauyin yaranta, sabida ubansu bai damu da karatun yaranshi ba, shidai Su fita shago su zauna mashi shine matsalarshi duk yaran haka,  Suke. 

        Gasu basu da, tarbiyar ko kad'an zagi abakinsu ko yaran maguzawa sai haka,  kuma duk formals sukawo zaginka zasuyi babu ruwansu kuma Iyayensu basu kwabarsu. 



          Duk cikin gidan basu tsoran kowa Sai Anas ta Abdulkadeer Suke musu gwale gwale, suma d'an babu matar da ta isa ya Sune maman Anas  bara shiga harkan kowa  duk rnar girkinta fa wuri take ta ajiyewa kowa,  nasa.


  

      A haka rayuwa tacigaba  da tafiya inda Yaran Mama suka sako kai,  Gashi suna same tarbiyare me kyau hankali da nutsuwa yana tare su,  haka ma Safeena duk cikin yaran gida  ita d'aya ce me zuwa makarantar Boko, gashi lokacin Allah ya jarabe Mama fa ciwo amma ba ya kwanta ba,  cikin  lokaci Kalilan  Safeenat ta gama primary,  wajen shiga secondary  aka fara samun  matsala  d'an lokaci guda, Yaran Mama suka juya  mata baya, inda suka saka idanunsu akan Safeenat suka  tsaneta, kuma suka daina bawa  mahaigiyarsu kulawa sai Hajiya fatu abinda yakara kasara Mama kenan, ciwo yashiga jikinta sosai. 

           Haka zasu zauna da Mama a d'aki sutura kuka, abin tausayin. 

           Gashi nan kusan komawa makaranta hankalin safeena yayi mugun tashi dan dole ta bazama cikin unguwar su ko xata same aikinyi cikin sa'a,  ya fad'a Gidan Malam  Abubakar Lada, dake suna shiga diban ruwa, har dakin Hajiya kaka taje ta zauna suka gaisa,  a hankali Safeenat  ta fara magana Hawaye na zuba daga idanunta  tace. 
"Hajiya Kaka, nazone don Allah ki taimakamin da aiki ko nawane ki biyani ina son biyan kudin makaranta ne, da kuma kud'in da zan na sayawa Mamana Magani idan ciwonta ya tashi.

*Page 9/10*
             '''Tsugune bata karewa mara abinyi ba'''



     Tabbas haka ne, masu iya magana suka ce, rai a ɓace hajiya fatu tabar ɗakin  tare da mugun nufi a ranta na sai tahana Maman Anas da Nanna farin ciki.

           Kiran Anas tayi ta labarta masa abinda Nanna take na aikatau, ya hanata dan zubda mutuncin gidane, zugashi tayi sosai, aikuwa yafita fuuu sai ɗakin Mamanshi yana shiga nanna tana haɗawa Mamansu abinci bankaɗe labulen yayi, cike da bala'i ɗago ido Mamansu tayi ta kalleshi sau ɗaya sannan ta karɓi abincin ta fara cin abinci.
  Jikin nanna ɗauka rawa yake, sabida tsoro tana kallon Anas 

"Ke zo nan" ya faɗa tare da ɓata fuska.
         "Bazaki je koina ba, zauna kici abincinki babu inda zaki dukkanku ban daki kowa ba dan haka kar wani ya taɓa min y'a idan kuwa mutum yaki ban yafe masa ba, duniya da lahira dan kaima abin duka kake amma ban dakeka ba toh kar wani ya taɓa min lafiyar yarinya dan y'acw ba baiwa ba." ta faɗa masa hankalinta kwance batare da wata damuwa."

     Jikinshi a sanyaye yabar kofar ɗakin, tun daga ranar Nanna tasame yanci anas ya daina dukanta mutune gidan suka tsirfo da wata fitina nason hanata, zuwa gidan Hajiya kaka aiki da zuwa makaranta nasata aiki wanda yake sakata makara.

      Nan ma, Maman Anas tasame Me kuɗin gero da maganar da dare bayan ya dawo yana zaune a tsakar gida taje tasame shi, gaisawa sukayi, tai shiru na wani lokaci kafin tace.
    "Alhaji nazo wajenka ne niman alfarma ki zan samu." Shiru tayi tana kallonshi,

          kashe raidiyon d yake sauraron b.r.c bauchi ya tashi ya zauna daga kishingiɗar da yayi sannan yace.

"Ina sauraronki Saratu meke tafe dake."

           Murmushi tayi sannan tace.
"Akan Nanna nazo gurinka, nasan kowa na da ikon akan yaranka, kuma a ɓangarena ban taɓa takura ɗan wata ko hantarsu ba, asalima ina gyara mu'amalata dasu sannan kafin wannan ina son kasan wani abu kaine ka hana asa mana abinci ko ni da Nanna? wannan bai dameni ba, dan Allah ya bani y'a ɗaya me zuciyar maza uku, tunda tana fita niman nakanta kuma Alhamdulillah an dace, toh abinda nake son na faɗa guda ɗaya ce kashiga tsakanina da matanka da yaranka, idan basu nk naci ba karsu hana min y'a fita tanima, kuma kar wata tasake samin safeena aiki idan kuwa haka ta faru wallahi nakara maka da tallahi ran kowa zai ɓaci daga yau sai yau Nanna bazata sake wanke wanke ba tunda babu, kason kwanonta abincinta a gidan nan wannan shine dokaciyata akan matanka da Y'ayanka "

      gyara zaman yayi cikin nutsuwa sosai yana kallonta sauke numfashi yayi sannan yace.
"Wannan shine matsalarki?" yajefa mata tambaya tare da tsareta da idanun yana mamakin yanda lokaci ɗaya tasake hali, abinda bataɓa yi bakenan, tsawon shekara 29 da suke tare ba taɓa ko korafi akan yaranta ba, sai yau.

          "Eh shine matsalata." tabashi amsa a takaice, kanta asunkuye.       
       "Shi kenan Allah yabaki hakuri batun hana abinci wallahi bansan da haka ba, amma bari naki Fatu da Zuwaira. Zuwaira!!! Fatu!!!"

           Tare suka fito ɗinkis ɗinkis, suka tawo har gurin me kuɗin gero suka zauna nan ya watsa musu tambaya me ye dalilinsu na hana maman Anas abinci.?
          Basu taɓa tsamanin haka ba, dan sunsan basu da wata hujja, tsaresu yayi da ido yana jiran jin me zasu ce.

        Kame-kame suka fara can da ya gaji ya daka musu tsawa, yace .
"Tambayarku nake kun tsaya kuna min kame kame, meye dalilin hana saratu abinci gidan nan."

"Ehto alhaji, wai munga bata girkine kuma, baka shiga wajenta mun zata ko ka sauwake matane, shi yasa muka daina saka mata abinci."

        Mamakine ya rufe shi jinjina kai yayi sannan yace.
"Yayi kyau toh daga yau kar wani yasake saka Safeenat aiki sannan tunda nace muku, Saratu ba matata bace toh daga yau kar wata tasake shiga hidimar yaranta,kuma abinci dare da rana, asaka mata idan kuwa na bincika nasamu ba haka ba wallahi zan sake mata ina sonta na auroku tunda bata da lafiya na sauke mata hidimar gidan da nawa kar takuma kawo karanku,"

             ran hajiya fatu ya ɓaci sosai haka suka mike cike da bakin ciki.

          Alhamdulillah, tun daga ranar kome yazowa Mama da Nanna sauki,.

Sabon salo...

Abinda suka fito da shi, shine kai sukan Nanna a gidan hajiya kaka nan ma basu samu mafakar siyasa ba, sai suka shiga ce mata karuwa😱   wannan kalmar yafi kome ɗagawa Mamanta hankali inda hawan jini yasake sakata agaba, tun anayi a cikin gida har yafita waje, wanda abin ya isa kunnen Malam lada nan yasa aka kira Me kuɗin gero yayi masa tass kuma yayi alkawarin duk wanda yasake faɗa mata, haka sai ya ɗaure iyayensu, shi kenan nan tasame sauki.

           Rayuwarsu ba'ace ba daɗi ba amma sai godiyar Allah.
   

         A hankali safeenat karatunta yake tafiya har ta isa aji uku, a wannan lokacin jikin Mamanta ya tsananta cikin wannan yanayi tabiya kuɗin 6-3-3-4, tana biyan kuɗin, cikin dare ranar ciwon mamanta ya tashi wanda yayi sanadin sumanta, cikin firgici tafito ta buga kofar babansu aka kaita, asibiti koda suka je take kowa suka watse har da yaranta nacikinta sai Safeenat lokacin shekarunta sha biyar,  koda aka rubuta mata abinda zata sayo babu, kuɗi kuka tasake a hankali take kuka, fita tayi daga asibitin lokacin kusan karfe ɗaya na dare, tun daga asibiti ta tako da kafa, har unguwar jaki.*(Ba zaku gane nisan asibitin zuwa unguwarsu safeenat ba, amma nasan yan cikin garin bauchi zasu ki manta nisan sabida akwai nisa don Allah idan ke yar bauchi ce kiyi min comments ta writerup, yanda zan gane akwai yan bauchi a online)* 


        Haka ta iso, ko tsoro babu a ranta har gidan baba ladan tazo ta buga Yahuza ne ya buɗe tashiga, har ɗakin Hajiya kaka ta buga daga ciki tace.
"Waye?"

       Cikin kuka tace.
"Hajiya nice Nannah."

        Sauka daga gadonta tazo ta buɗe mata kofa, tana ganin hajiya ta fashe da kuka, ta labarta mata halin da suke ciki asibiti, jikin hajiya asanyae tashiga ɗakin baba lada ta faɗa masa, ta faɗa masa abinda ya kawo Nannah,  shi da yahuza suka fito suka saka nan a gaba, zuwa asibiti nan yabiya kuɗin kome har da magani.

          Ba karamin kuka, Safeenat tayi ba ganin yanda kowa ke gudunsu, Eh toh jiki sai addu'a dan tunda aka kawota jiki yaki, satinta ɗaya.

      Ubangiji ya amshi rayuwarta da asuba, ranar juma'a, mutuwarta ya girgiza kowa har mijinta da ya wofantar da ita da yaranta, ɗauko gawar akayi zuwa gida aka mata jana'ida aka kaita makwancinta, Safeenat na kallonsu dan lokaci guda Allah ya ɗaura mata hakuri da juriya da dangana, 

         Bayan sati uku dakyar hajiya kaka tasakata a gaba ta koma makaranta inda mutuwar tasake mai da ita miskila, tana kiyayye abinda zai haɗata da mutune,

          Bayan shekara biyu, ta gama scndry, da taimakon Aunty Adama, takardunta sukayi kyau take Baba ladan ya biya mata kuɗin makarantar jinya na asibiti, sabida murna ranar har kuka sai da tayi.

        Tana fara karatu manema suka mata, caaa dan tana da farin jini, daman Baba ladan ya hanata sauraronsu sai ta gama, tana gamawa kuwa, ta fara school of nursing,, suka fito saurayin da tafara me suna abbas malami ne a makarantar yafito har angama kome, yace ya fasa sailuba yakeso babu yanda ta iya, aka maida auren kan sailuba, bata sake wani saurayin ba, har sai da takusan gama karanta suka haɗu da wani Huzaifa likitane A cikin asibiti, nan ma sai da magana yayi nisa ya koma  kan laweeza, wannan karon tayi kuka, har ta daina kula kowa, sai ynz da suka haɗu da Hamza shima kuma, kaman sauran haka ce zata faru.

         Cigaban labari.
   Dakyar ta gama wanke wanke sannan ta tashi ana kiran sallar mangariba, ɗakinta tashiga tayi

      _Mommyna Zee Mahadi Ga ɗan karamin page babu yawa da fatan kina cikin farin ciki a duk inɗa kike tare da kanena_😘

*Page 13*

Bayan ya dawo daga sayo maganin, yana shiga ɗakin yasameta tana waya, kasa kasa tana ganinshi ta kashe sake kallonshi tayi sannan ta sake wani sirrintacceyar murmushi wanda yasake sussuta Sadauki jagaf ya zauna a kujeran yana jinshi wani iri wanda bazai tantance meke shirin faruwa da shi ba, zuba mata ido yayi kafin ya maida mata da martanin murmushi kafin yace.
"Bansan dame zanyi miki godiya ba, b'cos you save my life(kin ceci rayuwata) da rayuwarki nagode."

           Cikin wni irin yanayi tamasa fari da idanunta, sannan ta sake murmushi wanda yayi sanadin lotsawar dimple point ɗinta ciki tace.
"Never mind,hmm wata macece bazata iya bada rayuwarta dan citon rayuwarka ba nd wani ya tɓa faɗa maka kana da kyau kuwa, sannan ana ganinka anga sadaukin maza auchiiiiiiii."

       "Sannu karki tashi koma ki kwanta nagode sosai da yabona, kuma sunana kenan Sadauki." ya faɗa mata tare da zuba mata ido ba zai iya cewa ga abinda yake mintsinninsa ba, amma yana jinta a cikin zuciyarsa.


      "Nice name amma babu ruwana dakai, tunda kai bazaka tambayi sunana ba sai ni keta surutu," karshe maganar tayi idanunta cike da hawaye, lumshe idanun tayi suka zubo akan lallausar kumcinta,


       Shi a rayuwarshi ba ma'abocin hayaniya bane, amma akanta gashi har murmusawa yake, ciro hanke yayi ya, mika mata tashare fuskarta cikin tattausar murya yace.
"Me sunan queen me saurin kuka,"
      A sangarce ko nace ashgwaɓe ta tura masa baki tana cewa.
"Bayan sai da nayi kuka aka tambaya naki wayo tunda nike ta haukana, akanka dan kaga kai me kyaune shi yasa kake ja min aji."    Girgiza kai yayi yana mamakinta shafa sajenshi yayi cikin nutsuwa sannan yace.
"Am sowiee, bazan kuma ba faɗa min ranki shi daɗe."

           Wani rausayar da kanta tayi sai da taja masa class sosai kafin tace.
"Yusrah."

        "Suna me daɗi," yace mata kallon agogon hanunshi yayi sannan yasake kallonta cikin rashin jin daɗi yace.
"Zan tafi amma har yanzun banga wani yazo daga gidanku ba, ko ba'a nan kike ba."

      Sunkuyar dakanta tayi kasa sannan ta fashe masa da kuka, murya kasa kasa, take kukan ga hawayen da suke zuba baji ba gani, komawa yayi kusa da ita ya zauna a bakin gadon fuskarshi ɗauke da tashin hankali ya rike hannunta yace.
"Kiyi hakuri banyi niyyar sakaki kuka ba, kinji don Allah bar kukan nan yana sami zuciyata jin zafi kiyi hakuri bazan sake ba."

             Shiga jikinshi tayi batare da ya ankara ba, sosai ta cusa kanta a kirjinshi tana matsar kwalla (wai nace ikon Rabbi) wato daman jiran haka take, shi kuwa ganin shi yasakata kuka, bai kawo kome ba, yashiga rarrashinta, dakyar sukayi sallama da ita akan gobe zai dawo, dan yasan matukar ya koma Barde bazai taɓa barinshi ya fito ba,

        Sadauki nafita tasake murmushi a hankali ta ɗauki wayarta tatura wani sako.

~~~~
Kaman da wasa Sadauki yake, kulawa da Yusrah dan ya ɗauketa kaman yar uwarsa ce, dan haka yake kokarin kyautata mata, yanda zai hanata shiga damuwa kwananta biyar aka sallameta, wani unguwa ya kaita a can suleja, anan take duk sati zai kawo mata ziyara suyita yawo asuleja, a wunin ranar sai dare yake komowa gida.

       Ganin yanda yake walwala yasa Iyayenshi da abokanshi suka san ya haɗu da mace dan ko wani taron sirri ake a gurin aikinsu sai an kwace wayarshi, baki ɗaya ya koma kaman sharukhan ya zama wani zautacce,

           Shak'uwace kawai yaja haka, koda suka fahimci juna yaso gabatar da ita amma taki tace.
"Saurin me kake bayan ina tare dakai kawai kasa a ranka ni mallakarkace dankaine abokin,rayuwata."

       Babu yanda ya iya,gashi ya faɗawa Adam da Barde zai gabatar da ita, sun kwashi wata biyar kafin soka furtawa juna kalma me daɗi a tsakaninsu sannan acikin wannan ranar da suka faɗawa junarsu haka a ciki ranar Yusra ta wargaza masa, farin cikinsa da yardan da yayi da ita badan Allah ya nufa yana da nisan kwana ba, da babu shi.

Abinda ya faru, kuwa bayan ya taso daga aiki yana hanyae komawa gida, lokacin kusan 8:09 dan sunyi meeting akan wani ɗan ta'addan da za'a kama kuma kome a hannun sadauki yake, kiranshi Yusra tayi tana kuka cikin tashin hankali, babu shiri ys juya zuwa suleja.

       Koda ya isa, gidanta har yashiga yasameta a falo ta rike kanta, gabanta yaje ya zauna ya dafa gwiwarta ya ɗago fuskarta yaga yanda sukayi gaje gaje da hawaye, faɗawa jikinshi tayi tana kuka. sai da tayi me isarta sannan ta mike ta ɗauko masa ruwa da juice ta zuba a kofi, ta bashi, zama tayi tacigaba da share kwallarta bayan yasha yace.
"Yusrah meke faruwa ? kin kirani kuma kin cigaba da kuka me ya farune." 

       Kamar wacce ya zageta haka tasake fashewa da kuka. Abin duk ya dame shi, ganin tafi son kukan yasa ya zuba mata ido, can bayan mintuna goma sadauki ya fara jin jiri da ganin kome na falon na juya masa, kafin wani lokaci ya zube a kujeran da yake zaune, mikewa tayi jikinta a sanyaye tashiga ɗakinta kanta a sunkuye tace.
"Uncle maganin ya fara aiki akanshi fa."

        Kallonta wani farin datijo tare da abokanshi da wasu mutane karti majiya karfi suka fito, sannan suka zagaye shi kallonta datijon yayi sannan ya kauda kanshi cikin ɓacinrai yace.
"Dashi da Bukar shuwa wakika fi so?"

       ɗago idanunta tayi cikin dakarkiyar zuciya tace.
"Nafi son Daddyna  ban taɓa son sadauki ba, kuma bazai so shi ba har karshen rayuwata."

                Kallon kwaryar idanunta yayi sannan yace.
"Dole kibada kanki dan musami hanyar da zamu ɓata masa suna  a gurin aikinshi kuma yanzun zakiyi dan maganin da aka saka a cikin lemon har da na karfin maza bashawaranki muke bukata rayuwar Bukar shuwa muke bukata, ku ɗauke shi zuwa ɗakinta, kuma ina son haka ya faru inda kuwa kika ki toh kisani keda mahaifinki har abada."

        Yanda take jin mahaifinta bata jin bazata iya aikata kome ba,gyaɗa kai tayi cikin tashin hankali ke kallon uncle ɗinta, ɗakinta aka kai sadauki, sannan itama tabi bayansun, tun da tashiga kasa aikata kome tayi tana kuka, sadauki ya rusa mata kome tunda ya kama mahaifinta, dan tana tsaye sai ga Uncle ɗinta yashigo ganinta a tsaye yasan haka, zata faru dan haka bai wani ɓata lokaci ba ya fesa mata, Wani farin hoda tuni ta zuɓe a gurin, ɗauƙarta yayi ya jefata a kusa da sadauki, sannan ya ja kofa yarufe yana ciki ganin duk yanda yaso sadauki ya nimeta amma karfin maganin yaki barshin yawancan  maganin yayi tasifi a jikknshi barcinshi yake tsakaninsa da Allah.

     Ran Salahudeen ne yakara ɓaci dan haka ya janyo sadauki daga gadon zuwa kasa nan ya hau gadon yayi nan yarabata da kayanta, sannan ya shiga bin ilahir jikinta da kallo a hankali yake bin boons ɗinta da harshenshi cikin mayunwaci bukatarta yashiga bin duk wani lungu da sako najinta yana, lasarsu cikin kwarewa da goguwa irinta mazan bariki, a hankali ya isa kasanta, yakai hanunshi yana wasa da ita🙈🙊 jin irin abinda yake mata yasa tafarka tashiga biye masa, duk da tana cikin nauyayyar barci, sannun a hankali ya gigitatata jikinshi narawa ya rabata da budurcinta sai da yayi tsalle akanta har sau uku, kafin ya sauka ya janyo sadauki ya maidashi gurin bayan ya rabasho da kayanshi, nan ya saka waya yayi record ɗin yanda ya kwantar dasu inda kagani zaka iya rantsiwa wani abu sadauki yayi mata bayan ya gama, ɗauƙarsu sannan ya fita yasa yaranshi suka buɗe motar sadauki, akasakashi, nan wani cikin yaran yaja motar bayan sun ɗauke laptop ɗinshi da wasu files wanda za'a kama wanda ake nima.

*Page 14*

Sai da suka kai motarsi tsakiyar titi kan gadar abuja sannan suka sa babbar mota suka murkusheshi, inda suka jefo shi daga sama Allah ne ya nufa Sadauki yana da nisan kwana, koda suka koma faɗawa Salahudeen sukayi abinda yasasu, nan ya ɗauki laptop ɗin yashiga dubawa, ko zai same wani bayani amma bai samu ba karshe  wayar Sadauki yasa min bayanin wanda za'a kaiwa sumame, ba kowa bane shine.

  A lokacin yabar yanki abuja da Yusrah lagos suka nufa, daga can yabi jirgin zuwa zuwa,Etophiea daga can yasake labarin ya gudu tare da taimakon Sadauki dan shi ya bashi labarin haka kuma yaronshine, hankalin Ma'aikatarsu sadauki ya tashi, nan aka bazama niman Sadauki dak'yar aka same shi a gindin gada, babu wanda ya kawo masa rayuwa.

        Asibiti aka kawoshi kusan satinsa biyu kafin yasan idan yake, yana samun sauki (NIA) suka makashi kotu, dakyar aka sallame karan bayan sun dakatar dashi dan baisan kome ba tun daga ranar Sadauki ya koma wani iri dan bai taɓa tsamanin Yusrah zata ci amanarshi ba, wato haɗa baki da ita akayi suka cuceshi.

    Wannan kenan....

Cike da farin ciki me kuɗin gero yashiga cikin gidanshi rike da alawa da goro, fuskarshi kunshe da farin ciki, yana shiga ya same matan gidan a zaune mikawa Fauxiya kayan yayi dansu basu san suga babba da kaya su amsa ba. Sai dai idan kaine zaka mika musu,

"Gashi ku rabawa makota na ɗaurin auren Uwata ce, duk da auren babu shiri amma anɗaga tarewa sai nan da wata biyu, kafin nan an gama mata kayan ɗakinta,"

"Alhaji wacece aka ɗaura mata aure."?

          Zuciyarshi ɗaya hankalinshi nakan kofar turakanshi ya buɗe sannan ya juya yace musu.
"Safeenatu mana,"

"Eyye!!!!" suka duki kirjinsu cike da tashin hankali suna kallonshi baki buɗe harta yaransu a firgice suke shigewa yayi ɗakinshi can ya leko yace.
"Aikuwa ɗan gidan barde ta aura jikan Malam ladan. ai na manta ɗan gidan sanata nake nufi."

"Lafiya kake kirana haka.?"

"Ina kuɗaɗena, da nake baku ajiya, kuma ina Mamana take dan ke zan tambaya da tun daga ranar da Kika bani zobo masha ranar da nasayo maganin Mama ban sake tuno kome ba sai yau."

     Zuba mata idanun yayi yana jiran me zata ce, amma ina hankalinta yayi masifar tashi nan kuwa ya haukace mata,

     ~~~~
A gidn Hajiya kaka bayan ɗaurin auren da akayi Hajiya kaka tabawa Nanna babbar sabon mayafi ita dai bata san meke faruwa ba, turata falon Baba ladan na cikin gida tayi inda taje ta gaida Barde cike da kunya farin ciki fal a zuciyar shi wanda fuskarshi ya gaza ɓoyewa kallonta yayi sannan yace.

             Shiru tayi kafin ta kalle zuwaira tace.
"Kin fahimci abinda yake shirin faruwa kuwa, inda baki manta ba abinda na dungulbi yace duk ranan da Nanna tayi aure abinda mukayi Mamansu da Yaranta xai lalace shi yasa duk wanda yazo nimanta nake, juyar musu da tunani kan yarana gashi kuma wanda ta aura iyayenshi masu kuɗine da zai sake yar banza yazo ya auri faiza da yafi min Wallahi."

"Taɓɗijam, kina nufin zan cigaba da kallonkine nima da zan samu nima ɗaya daga cikin yarana zai aura eyye. Kowa kansa yasani nima haka." ta faɗa haɗi da hura hancinta, fita tayi daga ɗakin..

          Mimi ce ta mike tace.
"Ni na dace dashi ba kuba."

     Kafin kace kabo rigima ta kaure a ɗakin wanda yaja aka fara ba raini, Kowacce tana ihun ita ta dace da sadauki.

Koda Barde ya koma gida ya fadawa, Hajiya Abida, ba karamin daɗi taji ba, dan dama tana son Nanna sai gashi Allah ya kawo mata ita gida,

" Tunda har yanzun baka samu ba, toh munyi namu ikon mun sama maka mata kuma an ɗaura auren da fatan zaka iya rike matar da muka zaɓa maka, dn yana da hankali da nutsuwa."

"Barde ai da kayi shawara dani, sai ka yanke min hukunci lokaci ɗaya gaskiya nikam....."

     Wani katon harara barde yasake masa kafin ya gyara rikon remoter ɗin hanunshi yace.
"Toh ubana banyi shawari da kai ba kayi hakuri karka min baki, kuma ko da wasa kayiwa Yarinyar mutane rashin mutunci zan saɓa maka wallahi kana jina ko tashi, kuma kashirya Next week zakaje bauchi."

       Dakyar ya buɗe baki yace.
"Afuwa barde bazan sake maka musu ba."

"Allah yayi maka albarka tashi kaje" ranshi a ɓace yabar palon yana kitsima irin rashin mutunci da zaiwa Yarinyar rashin arzuki,

       Da yamma likis lokacin ankai kayan gidansu Nanna, ita kuma ta sauka a napep kenan sai gasu Sadauki a mota, zasu shiga unguwar cikin rashin sa'a wani datijo yasha gabansu saura kiris su bigeshi Allah ya taimaka da taimakonta ta sha gabansu sadauki da ruwan bala,i suns cikin mota, ranshine ya ɓaci ya fito dan ya lura kaman Hakurin da Adam yake bata kaman zukagata yake, aikuwa yafitowa ya iso gabanta, ganin yanda yakereta tsayi yasa tace.
"Baba keke muje ka barsu da Allah haka suke basu da imani da tausayi."

"Karamar mara kunya kawai."

"Eh ɗin me kiran samudawa kawai"

   Tunda suka dawo bauchi yake tufka da warwara yanda zai koyawa Safeenat hankali har suka tashi a gurin aiki, yana cikin mota kira yashigo masa, yana dubawa yaga Yusra, faɗuwar gaba yaji, kafin ya ɗaga.

"Ai na zata baxaka ɗauka bane daman zan faɗa maka ne, na kusan zuwa kuma dan kai zanzo da fatan zaka tanadar min da abinda zaka min...." tana gama faɗin haka ta kashe wayar,

       dafe goshi yayi tabbas yayi kuskure amma taya zai gyara firstly shine yasan yanda zai lallaɓa yusra dan ya lura so take ta, sake gefashi cikin wani tashin hankali, haka ya isa gida jiki amace.

     ~~~~
South Africa.......
A tsaye take tana zana hoton  Sadauki riga da wndo ne a jikinta tasake gashinta a baya tana zana shi cike da farin ciki, tunda akasata tayi aikin akashi da farko babu kaunarshi a cikin ranta har zuwa lokacin da Uncle ɗinta ya faɗa mata, abinda ya faru da ita, kuma tasan da haka amma take juyashi son ranta kafin tace ya aureta kuma yasan yanda za'a sake mata mahaifinta.

        Murmushi take tana gyara fuskarshi.

"My dota kina nan ne, ga juice ɗinki karɓa kisha."

     "Yes uncle ina zana hoton wnca Agent ɗin ne, kasan yanda nake ji dashi amma yanzun zan ajiye,"

       ajiye brush ɗin tayi ta amshi cup ɗin glass din da yake hanun kawunta kafa kai tayi tasha sannan ta ɗago tace.
"Uncle ina enjoy ɗin wannan drink ɗin fa, yana da daɗi sosai."

       Wani munafinki murmushi yayi sannan yacigaba da shan nashi suna hira da ita.

       Can bayan mintuna goma tashiga layi gaban allon sa take zana hoton Sadauki ta tsaya tana kallonshi fuskarta ɗauke da dariya tace.
"Sadauki kansan yanda nake sonka kuwa, ina masifar sonka bazan iya rayuwa babu kai ba, na kusan zuwa gareka,"

      Ran uncle ne yayi mugun ɓaci duk irin abinda yake mata bata gani ba, hankalinta yana wani guri can zuwa gareta yayi zai dafata ta juya, sak taga ya koma mata Sadauki kaman zata faɗi taje ta rungume shi, tace.
"Ina sonka Sadaukina....." ɓingirewa tayi ta fara barci sosai.

          Daukarta yayi cak yayi wajen ɗakinshi da ita, nan ya rabata da kayanta yashiga hidimarshi da ita sai da yaji ya gamsun da ita sannan ya ɗauketa ya kaita ban ɗaki yayi mata wanka sannan ya dawo ya maida mata kayanta.......


       ~~~~
Sannun snnun bai hana zuwa sai dai daɗe ba'a jeba, wata kusan biyu ake nima, Aunty Adama ta ɗauke Nanna tana mata gyara musaman, inda akai mata gyaran fata sai ɗan abinda aka bata, kaɗai kuma kullum yin gyara ake aikinta ma daga can take zuwa....

           

      Ana saura kwana biyu ta tare ta dawo gidansu, nan taga wani sabon sauyi daga hajiya fatu, Gidan lallai Aunty Baraka tazo ta kaita, gidansu ya cika da mutum dangin Mamansu da na Babansu.


    Washi gari.

Sukayi waleema ita da yan gidansu, da safe dan za'a kaita Allah sarki me kuɗin gero duk da kaunarshi da kuɗinsa sai da yayi mata kaya irin na alfarma nafita kunya......

*Page 17*
    

Anyi waleema cikin kwanciyar hankali, sannan aka ɗauki amarya zuwa gidan baba ladan, daga nan suka ɗauketa sai jahun  inda aka haɗa tawagar ka amarya.

         Tunda Safeenat taga sun fara barin bauchi ta kifa kanta a a tsakiyar cinyoyinta tana kuka me tsuma rai yau take kewar Mamanta, sosai take jin maraicinta. Kuka take sosai wanda yaja hankalin kanwar Mamanta tashiga rarrashinta har tayi shiru.

      Basu isa abuja ba, sai karfe shida suna isa Ummi ta karɓesu hannun bibiyu tare da abin motsa baki. 

  Bayan sallar isha aka shiryata tsaf, zuwa dinner tayi kyau sosai ga kayan da tasaka dark green ne, kome masha Allah haka ma ta ɓangaren ango shima ba laifi dan yayi shiga irinta bahaushe, kuma abin yayi ba laifi.


        Ko a gurin dinner sai da suka raba hali inda nanna takalle Sadauki ta fashe da dariya mara sauti, a hankali take dariya bayan tarufe bakinta.

             Kallon yayi kafin ya sake wani murmushin mugunta, kai hannunshi yayi gefen cikinta dake rigar bata kamata ba, dan basu san size ɗinta ba, ya kaimata mintsini, duk abinda suke akan idanun Adam wanda yake dariya tsakaninshi da Allah.

          A firgice taso mikewa ya maida ita yasake mata murmushi, kwalla ne ya cika mata idanun kanshi yakai kunnenta yace.
"Haba da wuri, kinga mutane suna kallonmu kawai haɗiye kukanki dan yanzun muka fara."
  
      Yana yakai kashen maganarshi ya hura mata iska a kunnen danne hawayen dasuke shirin zuwa daga idanunta yayi tana me yake hakoranta.

     Kamar tana murmushi yayi kawai dan ya gama shirya mata wulakanci ba kaɗan ba. Koda aka zo yanka cake, ita fara dan haka da gayyai ta yanko katon cake wanda yafi bakinsa ta tusa mashi tana dariya, me Adam zaiyi banda dariya shikan burgeshi suke mutane sun koma like tom & jerry.

        Dakyar sadauki ya haɗiye cake ɗin yana kallonta, amma kasan rashinshi zafi yake masa but idan kaga fuskarshi zaka ɗauka farin ciki yake da abinda tayi masa.


       Koda akace ya yanko nashi cake ɗin ya bata karami ya yanko yabata by mistake hannunshi ya shiga bakinta ta aikuwa ta haɗa da hannunshi ta gutsira masa cizo, yarfe cake ɗin yayi a jikinta yana yarfe hannunshi dan ya ji zafi sosai murmushi Alla kara tayi masa, mamakin yanda tayi ta masa abu yake, hmmm yace a ranshi.

          
          Har aka shi sadauki bai kulata ba,daga wajen gidansu aka maidata, shi da Adam suka mike zuwa gidan..

         Kallonshi Adam yayi sannan ya maida hankalinsa kan hanya duk da baiga alamar sakin fuska ba amma dole ya taushi abokinsa dan ya lura ranshi yayi mugun ɓaci....

      A hankali ya fara magana da cewa.
"Abubakar kayi hakuri da abinda Safeena tayi maka nagani da idona, kuma nasan tayi maka ba dai-dai ba, amma karkayi amfani da wannan abinda tayi ka rama, mace ce tana raunin tunani, matukar kace zaka biye mata toh wata rana karyata zakayi dan haka nake baka hakuri da abinda tayi kuma ai bawata babba bace dan, bazata wucce 21yrs ba kenan dole kayi hakuri da ita...."


      Juyewa Sadauki yayi ya kalleshi fuskarsa ɗauke  da murmushi, yace.
"Karka damu da haka, babu abinda zai faru.."

        Tunda ya faɗi haka har suka isa gidan, ta kofar baya ya shiga, jan mota adam yayi yana girgixa kai, wallahi tsab yasan sai Sadauki ya rama abinda tayi masa.


     Haka tafi gida cike da tunanin Sadauki da Safeenat,

           Koda sadauki ya isa part ɗinsa zuɓe yayi akan cushion ɗin falinshi yana maida numfashin gajiya ya jima sosai kafin ya shiga ɗakinshi kayan jikinshi ya rage sannan ya faɗa wanka, bayan ya gama yafito kugunshi ɗaure da towel, tsane kanshi yayi sannan yasaka riga da wando, komawa falo yayi ya duba flask yaga akwai ruwan zafi.

        Lipto ya haɗa da yasa low sugar da limen tsami, ya zauna yana sha a hankali yana nazarin abinda Safeenat tayi masa, runtsi idanushi yayi ransi nasake ɓaci dakyar ya gama shan tea ɗin ɗakinshi ya koma ya kwanta.

      Basu shigo gidan ba sai karfe takwas na dare, nasiha Adam yayi musu da suyi hakuri da juna, sannan suka fito da Nazifa rakosu Nanna tayi zuwa falo sau ɗaya ta kalle fuskar sadauki bata sake marmarin sakewa ba, har bakin gater suka rakosu.

       rufe gidan yayi yashigo, ɗakinshi ya nufa ya faɗa wanka yana gamawa yafito, saka kaya yayi yafito zuwa ɗakinta, koda yazo ya rike handle ɗin kofar yaji a rufe buga kofar yayi cike da ɓacin rai, yace.

Ihu tasaka sosai sannan tashiga fatali da duk wani abinda yake gurin tana farfasa na fasawa, duk wani abu me amfani sai da ta tarwatsasu, sannan ta koma gefe ra fashe da kuka sosai ta haɗa kanta da gwiwarta a tacigaba da kukanta,

*Page  19*
     

Kamar kowani dare da Yusra tasa kanta na zuwa club da shaye² kana ganinta kaga Rainon Bariki, amma yau buguwar nata da sauki dan batawani bugu ba, tana shiga gidan tasame Kawunta a falo shida wasu abokan harkanshi, ko kallo basu isheta ba ta wuccesu sai ɗakinta ko kayanta bata, cire ba balle shoe ɗinta rub da ciki ta kwanta sai barci,

       a falo kuwa kaman yayi ihu sabida kishi dan ga lura tun ranar da yanuna mata hoton bikin Sadauki tagama haukacewa.
        Ga wani baturen da yaketa yaba suranta, dan irin short gown ɗin nan ne, iya cinyantar ruwan Orange,haka ma shoe ɗinta kalar kayanta sai pose ɗinta, tayi kyau sosai ga uban karin da tasaka a kanta, after da tana da gashinta bata buƙatar kari..

         Sai wajen 2am suka bar gidan, cikin azama da nufin ya durjeta ƴanda yasaba yashiga ɗakin.
       Juyata yayi cikin nutsuwa yana kare mata wani irin kallo cike da kwaɗayinta lashe bakinshi yayi ya sauka ya cire kayanshi, sannan ya koma yashiga tura mata rigarta sama, dake bata bugu ainun ba, firr ta buɗe idanunta sai akanshi a razane ta hankaɗeshi ya faɗi kasa sannan ta janyo blankes ɗin gadon ta rufe jikinta cike da tsoro da mamaki ta koma can kuryar gadon, muryarta na rawa tace.
"Uncle me nake gani haka, kace min mafarki nake ba ido biyu ba,"

         Mikewa yayi cikin azama ya nufeta da mugun nufi, aikuwa suka shiga kokuwa cikin tashin hankali, yayinda yake niman biyar bukatarshi ko ta halin ƙaƙa, ita kuma ta kafe ta darje bazata bashi abinda yake kulafucin samu ba, dakyar yasamu nasaran jefata kan gadon zazzafe yake aika mata da kayan aikinsa, sai da gama kashe mata jiki, kare mata kallo yayi da nufin ya isa, Last stage, kawai sai jin karan fashewar kwalba akanshi sannan, tasa kafarta da takalminta ta mauje kayan aikinshi😹 ihu yasaka, ya tuntsira can gefe guda ya suma tana ganin ya suma, asukwane ta sauka daga gadon ta buɗe wardrob ɗinta, shiga haɗa kayanta, sannan taje ɗakinshi ta kwashi kuɗi me ɗinbin yawa, sannan tafita daga gidan, masu kula da gidan sun ganta amma basu hanata fita ba.

"Me kika faɗawa, Aunty Adama wallahi idan kika kuskura kika shiga tsakanina da Iyayena, zan sauya miki halitta zan baki mamaki, ki iya bakinki ki tauna maganarki kafin ki faɗa kuma wallahi kika kuskura kika karya tani a gaban ummyna zan baki mamaki wucce muje, da idanunki kamar ƙazar mayu."

       Har yayi nisa yasake dawowa fita yayi daga motar tazo gabanta kafin ta ankara ya taɓa tsakanin wuyarta da kunnenta zuɓewa tayi a gurin tasamu, boot ɗin motarshi ya buɗe ya jefata a ciki, sannan yaja motar.


      Gidanshi ya nufa da ita, yakaita ɗakin dake bayan gidan Bq ya ajiyeta yayi tafiyarshi.

        Yana shiga falon wayarshi na kara ɗagawa yayi cikin ladabi ya kara a kunne, a hankali yace.
"Toh gani nan zuwa."

         Ko ɗaki bai shiga yasake fita, a motarshi ya kira wani nomber yace.
"Sir ina son abani dama ta karshe akan mission ɗin Alhaji bukar shuwa zanzo koda yaushe."

      Shiru yayi hannunsa ɗaya na tuki ɗaya yana waya cewa yayi.
"Sir last chance zaka bani wallahi xanyi amfani da damar dana samu don Allah ka bani wannan damar, amma idan kana ganin zan takuraku ba damuwa kubar abinku." Katse kiran yayi yaja karamin tsaki,


     sannan yacigaba da tafiya har ya isa gidansu, tun daga kofar falo yake jin muryan mutane shiga yayi jikinshi a mace duk da daman Baban Adam yakirashi yace gaskiya bazai nima mashi auren yarinyar ba, sabida bata taso a gaban mace ba sai a gaban namiji kuma tashafe shekara biyu a wata uwa duniya ba tare da wani nata ba sai abokin babanta shima ana nimanshi wanted.

          Dalilin kenan da yasa aka fasa niman auren, da sallama ya shiga falon amsa sallamar sukayi sannan suka zuba mashi idanun har ya isa cikin falon gaishesu yayi sannan ya zauna sunkuyar da kanshi yayi sannan yace.
"Abba gani" ya faɗa a takaice.

         Baba Abdullahi kanin barde yace.
"Sadeeq munje niman aurenka amma Allah bai nufa ba, Amma Babanka da yayi maka ruwa da tsaki zai maka karin bayani."

            Har Baba Abdullahi ya gama magana bai ɗago ka ba.

    
     Baban Adam ne ya amsa da cewa.
"Eh abinda Abdullahi ya faɗa maka haka ne, na janye niman ne sabida wasu dalilai daga ciki shine yarinyar bata da kyakyawan tarbiya sabida ta girma a gaban namiji kuma baza'a kirata da mace tagari ba, sai abu nagama bayan kama babanta da akayi bata da dangine da zata bi, Wani dabsn wata duniya, wannan sune dalilin da ya hanani amma idan kana ra'ayin kara mata Babanka Yusif yabaka yarshi Surraya, wacce take masters ɗinta a nan bauchi kuma bawani abu ake bukata a gurin mace sai nagarta da nutsuwa takasance nagari."


           Shiru yayi kanshi akasa sai da Baban Adam yasake mashi magana yace.
"Ko anshiga rayuwarkane a fasa maganar."

        D'ago kai yayi cikin ladabi yace.
"Allah yasa haka shine mafi alkhairi nagode ma haka."

       Murmushin da baikai zuci ba barde yayi dan sam baya kaunar sadauki yace zai kara aure a wannan shekarun nashi ina shi ina mata biyu haka suka gama tattaunawarsu tambayi barde ko anshiga hakkinshi, murmushi yayi sannan yace.
"A'a ai sadeeq ɗankune ni babu nawa a ciki kome kukayi ɗaya ne Allah ya tabbatar da Alkhairi."


       Da haka aka rufe tattaunawa bakinsu suka tafi gidan bakin Barde shima sadauki shiga ciki yayi suka gaisa da Ummy har barde ya shigo, ranshi a ɓace.
"Sadeeq wai me kake  nufi da tara matane ina zaka kai Surraya yarinyar bata da aibu amma hajiya Rabi ai fitina ce me kake sone a rayuwarka, yaushe kayi auren da har kake niman kara wata toh nidai nawa idanun Allah yabaka sa'a."


              Cikin tashin hankali ummy ta zaro idanun tace.
"Yar hajiya rabi, haba Sadeeq..."

      Kaman zaiyi kuka yace.
"Don Allah kuyi hakuri kuma ai ba yanda zance bana son yar Baba Abdullahi ba tunda kaga yana gurin Don Allah kutayani da Addu'a..." Yanda yayi maganar sai yabasu tausayi hakurin dole sukayi  sannan  suka  mashi nasiha, bayan sun gama ummy tace.
"Kazo min da Safeena a weekend kaji ko." gyaɗa kai yayi sannan yayi musu sallama yana hanya aka kirashi, ɗaukar wayar yayi ys tsume fuska yace.
"Yes, ok"

"Yallaɓe ina son kaduba matsalar sadauki fa, asalima kaman babu abinda ya shiga tsakaninshi da Safeenat babu alamun soyayya a tsakaninsu kaduba maganar nan fa."

      "Abida ki barshi da kanshi zai nimeta Allah ya taimaka ynz idan mukayi wani yunkuri za'ace dan ba ɗaki ɗaya muka fito da Abdullahi ba, kinga karshe yan ubanci zai fito kawai mucigaba da samu addu'a Amma gaskiya safeenat itace ta dace da Sadauki amma kome da kika gani yana cikin tsarin ubangiji kar muyiwa ubangiji shishigi."

Tsabagin tashin hankali bai san lokacin da yafito waje ba, yana yawo ya rasa meke masa daɗi a rayuwarshi, zama yayi a wani gurin shan iska da yake gidan, har aka kira sallah.

 Sake gyara zamanshi yayi cikin nutsuwa kafin yace.

           " ya isa haka kubarshi yayi aure"   
       
     Farin ciki fal zuciyar Sadauki da murna, zama alhaji tahir yayi sannan ya kalle sadauki yace.
"Ka tabbatar zaka iya adalci ko, idan zaka iya zan tsaya maka kodaAhmadu ba zai amince ba,yar waye."


     Sunkuyar dakai yayi cikin nutsuwa ya faɗa musu iyayenta, amma ya ɓoye musu asalin mahaifinta.

           "Shikenan kaje zan same Ahmadu da maganar kaji zan tabbatar ya aminci Insha Allah cikin sati me zuwa zamu tafi." 

         Da kwarin gwiwa sadauki yayi musu sallama,



          Tashin hankali wanda ba'a sama sa rana, koda Iyayen sadauki suka same labarin aurenshi ɓoye ɓacin ransu sukayi sannan suka bawa Alhaji Tahir goyan baya inda aka nemi wasu daga cikin yan uwansu da abokansu suka tafi niman auren Yusrah.Bayan tafiyarsu ne, Ummy takira sadauki, duk da yana tsoro bai hana shi zuwa ba.


    Zama yayi a gabanta cikin nutsuwa kanshi a sunkuye yace.

      ɗago kanshi yayi da sauri sannan ya sauke sabida kunyar maganar da tayi, itama maganar yayi mata girma amma sabida wutar ɓacin ran da yake cinta dalilin aurenshi.
          Tace.

          Marairaice fuska yayi sannan ya sunkuyar da kanshi bayan ya sassauta muryan shi yace.
"Ummy kiyi hakuri karki rabani da Safeenat wallahi ina son zama da ita kawai ina son haka sabida wani dalilina don Allah ku amince,"  yanda yake maganar ne sai yabata tausayi kuma tsakanin ɗa da Uwa sai Allah,


     "Shikenan Allah baku zaman lafiya," tace masa, tana gama faɗin haka tashi tayi tabar mashi falon dan ba ita za'ayiwa kishiya ba amma tafi wacce za'ayiwa kishiyan damuwa da tashin hankali.

*Page  23*

   Janyo wani kujera yayi yanda zai na kallon fuskarta ɗago fuskarta yayi sannan yayi murmushi, mikewa yayi ya ɗauko wani karamin box ya ajiye agabanta sannan ya zauna sunkuyar dakanshi yayi kasa kafin yace.
"Kisan me yasa na shanye duk abinda kike min, kisan me yasa ban kula haukarki ba."

         Dariya yayi sannan ya cigaba da cewa..
"Sabida ban taɓa son wata y'a mace ba sai ke, yanda kika bada rayuwarki dan ki ceci tawa yasa na mace akanki, ashe kuskurene ai tun ranar da kika kareni ya dace na jefa ayar tambaya akanki, banyi haka ba sai na faɗa sonki ashe ke da wani kudiri aranki, danasan ke yar Bukar shuwa ce da tuntuni na yakinceki, a rayuwata a madadin haka sai na haukace akanki ashe an turokine dan ki cutar da zuciyata, waye ya turo ki faɗa min asauwake nima zan miki adalci na sauwake miki amma idan kikayi taurin kai....."
     Ɗ'aga kafaɗarshi yayi alamun ko a jikina da abinda zai faru dake.
Buɗe box ɗin yayi sosai sannan ya shiga cire sharp object ɗin ciki yana jerasu a gabanta ɗaye salater ɗin yayi sannan ya kalli cikin idanunta yace.
"Tunda na fahimci abinda kuka min nasan an turokine kuma na gane me ake nufi da haka kinsan wata nawa na ɗauka akwance asibiti, kinsan illar maganin da kuka jefa min aruwan da nasha, Yusra kin zalunun ceni sosai a dalilin mugun maganin da kuka jefa min aruwan da kika bani ranar ya janyo min gaggarumin matsala, wanda ba lallai bane nayi farin ciki kamar yanda kowa yake farin ciki ko iyayena basu san illar dakuka min ba amma na ɗauka a kaddarata idan kika faɗa min gaskiya zan kyaleki ki tafi dan kema sakaki akayi amma wallahi idan baku faɗa min ba zan yanke yatsatsin hannunwanki baki ɗaya kuma zan miki abinda kuka min shine dokata ina jikinki waye yasaki."

"go head idan kika min karya zan karasa cire sauran ba tare da naji tausayinki ba."

    Bakinta narawa hawayen azaba yana zubo mata jan majina tayi kafin tace.
"Salahudeen abokin babana shi ya turoni, shine yasa nasaka maka maganin s cikin ruwa wallahi ni dai ce min yayi maganin barcine bansan na meye ba shine yasace bayanan sirrinka yasake bayan shi kuke target wallahi nima yayi amfani danine dan na ɗauki fansan mahaifina amma haka ni ban cutar dakaiba.. "

          Dukar table ɗin dake gabanta yayi yakama gashin kanta yarike dakarfin tsiya yace.
"Kisan maganin da yajefa min acikin ruwan ya kashe min Kwayoyin halittata na ɗa namiji, kuwa ko ance miki bana son haihuwane wallahi sai na baki mamaki kaman yanda aka sakaki kika cutar da zuciyata tare da nakasani haka kema zan miki."

    Jefar da ita yayi can gefe guda, yayi yana hucci, wani sringe ya ɗauka tare da wani ruwan alura yaja ya isa gabanta ya ɗago kujeran ya danna mata alluran a gefen hannunta, yace.
"Bazan kasheki ba, amma kije da allura sannan ina wancan matsiyacin yake yanzun..."

           Hawayen dasuka sami mafakar siyasa a idanuntane suka zubo shar tace.
"Yana south africa."

         Tattara kayanshi yayi yafita daga ɗakin ya rufeta, cikin gida ya nufa, yana shiga ya rike kanshi sosai, mikewa yayi ya faɗa ban ɗaki ya ɗauro alola snnan yazo ya tadda sallah bayan ya idar ya jima sosai yana addu'a sannan ya kwanta dan dare ya raba sosai.

   ............
A bangaren Nanna kuwa tana jin fitarshi da dawowarshi dan lokacin tana zaune akan abin sallah, tana kaiwa Allah kukanta, jin motsin shiyasa ta kwanta akan abin sallar. Kiran sallar farko ya tasheta alola tasake ɗaurowa tazo ta gabarta da ra'atul fijir,  tana addu'a aka fara salla asauran maslatai itama ta mike kenan taji *abin mata* yaso mata, kaman ta rusa ihu dan dole tashiga ban ɗaki ta gyara jikinta, sannan tazo takwanta dake juma'ace  bata da aiki, kwanciya tayi tana rike da casbi har barci yayi gaba da ita.

Washi gari a bauchi.......
    A dutsen tanshi unguwarsu Surayya kwance take tana barcin safiya Hajiya rabi tashiga ɗakinta zama tayi a kusa da ita dan tun jiya Alhaji Abdullahi ya faɗa mata ya bada auren surayya wa sadauki kuma abinka da me kishiyoyi bata son yartata ta botsere mata shi yasa tazo har dakin kafin wani ya shaida mata ɗan buga kafaɗarta tayi tace.
"Sannun mage tashi sarkin son jiki ke baki gaji da barcine ehhe tashi nazo miki da wani abin arzikine, kinji yar albarka"

, mika tayi sosai tare da salati kafin ta mike banɗakin tashiga tayi brush sannan tazo ta zauna cikin girmamawa tace.
"Hajiya ina kwana,"

         Farin cikine ya rufe hajiya rabi tace.
"Lafiya lau, yar albarkata yawa bansan yanda zaki ɗauki maganar ba amma nasan zaki kere duk wata yarinya dake cikin gidan nan idan sukaji labarin ke zaki aure ɗan Ahmadu wato Sadeeq sadauki jiya Abbanki ya badake, kuma yanda na fahimta baza'aja bikin da nisa ba, sabida yan bani na iya karsu mana kafar angulu, don Allah darling karkice a'a." 

        D'ago kananun idanunta tayi cike da mamaki take kallon Babartata kafin tace.

"Hajiya taya zan bijire muku bayan kuka haifeni don Allah ki bar rokona sai dai ni gaskiya ina jin kunyar Safeenat dan nasan yarinyar tana da mutunci tunda muna haɗuwa a gidan Baba ladan, kuma Hajiya keda bakya shiri da Ummy Sadauki kece zaki yarda ya aureni hajiya baki kula wani abu b...." fau taji an make bakinta ɗan karamin nan(My catty anshan maka.😹)
          Dakuwa hajiya rabi tai mata a fusace tace.
"Kinci kaniyarki,nice nake da nufi da auren wai ina ganin arziki yazo gida kice zaki saka kafa kituro min baga Mamu ba ba a ɗakinsu inda itace waye zai ji kanta da hajiya Jummai wato bari ki baɗa min kasa a ido shine zaki kunyatani nagode Surayya."

     Mikewa tayi zata bar ɗakin, da sauri surayya tarike hanunta tace.
"Hajiya ki yafe min na amince amma kinsan ina da Aliyu wnda nakeso amma zanyi muku biyayya hajiya." Shafa kanta tayi sosai tana saka mata albarka sannan tafita.
        Surayya na ganin hajiya tafita ta fashe da kuka dan ita kam wallahi bata son ɗan lukutiti, gwara aliyunta ɗan siriri kaman ita dan itama ba aukine da ita ba. Shigowar Mamu ce ɗakin yasa ta goge hawayenta ta kakaro murmushi amma dake mamu tasan labarin dariya tayi sannan tace.
"Da alamun dai sadauki ya kwacewa Dawood kenan auwo Jabir,,,A,a Aliyu ayya ai jiya Abba babba yake faɗawa Ummanmu an badake"(Wato Babansu Mamuh kenan dan suna gida ɗayane da Baban surayya amma dukkansu kannen Bardene Ummansu Mamuh itace Hajiya jummai suna zuba takara da Hajiya rabi Da da ummy suke kafin su bar dutsen tanshi suka koma gida dubu shine gasar ya koma kan hajiya jummai, duk yan uwan barde suka da mata biyu zuwa uku shine dai baida ra'ayin kari shiyasa sauran matan suke cewa yar Malamai ta shanye shi tunda yaki kara aure.

         Ganin yanda kawarta kuma yar uwarta take kuka itama ya taɓa zuciyarta dakyar ta rarrasheta tayi shiru, na suka shiga wani hiran dabam...


       ~~~~~~
Tasamu tayi barcin awa uku cur kafin wani masifafen ciwon ciki ya tasota a gaba wanda tun tanayi a hankali har yasa ta faɗo daga gadon sabida mugun ciwon ciki, tayin amma bai kai wannan ba, birgima take sosai a kasa tana buge duk wani abunda yake kusa da ita.

      Ƴazo wuccewa kicin yaji karan buge buge, yaje haka yazo zai koma yaji abinda yaji ɗazun shiga ɗakin yayi da sallama ganin yanda take birgima rike da cikinta ya bankaɗe kofar da karfin tsiya ya shiga ɗagota yayi yaga hawaye nazuba daga idanunta yace.
"Kee meye haka zakina birgimaa kasa haka."

         Rike hannunshi tayi takai kan maranta da ya kule yana murɗanta yaji ya ɗauki ɗume sosai, ɗaukarta yayi cak ya maida ta akan gado ya ɗauko hijab ɗinta yasaka mata, fita yayi ya buɗe motarshi da me lura da gater ɗin gidan na gannin shi yayi maza yazo ya buɗe mashi, komawa ciki yayi ya ɗaukota ya sakata a cikin motar sannan ya figeta da karfin tsiya, dan yana tsoron kar wani abu yasameta iyayenshi su mashi ba daɗi.

         Asibitin da take aiki nan ya kaita suna ganin itace kuwa drs maza da mata suka rufa mata dan masifa hana maza yayi su taɓata wai sai mata..........

*Page 25*


     Ta ɗauki lokaci tana sakawa da warwarewa, har ta gaji ta bar falon, side ɗin Sadauki aka buɗe mata a gyara yake kome na cikin wajen fess ne, A falon ta kwanta tana tuna rayuwarta dana mahaifiyarta, hawaye masu ɗume suka zubo daga gurbin idanunta.


           A hankali barci yayi nasaran saceta.

............
Alhamdulillah sadauki ya same nasaran kama Salahudeen tare da wasu mukarabansa kuma an makasu kotu.

      Yana dawowa gida suka tsunduma hidimar biki baji ba gani,  ba kama hanun yaro zuwanshi bauchi har sau biyu wai zance yaje.
   
    Sabida gajin hakuri irin na hajiya rabi, kiran ummy da bala'i wai ya ba'a kawo lefe ba, surayya na banɗaki taji katoɓaran da uwarta take bata san lokacin da tafito da gudu takwace wayar baki ɗaya ta kashe cikin tashin hankali tace.
 Hajiya me yasa kike min haka ne, ai zubda mutuncine taya zaki kira Uwar wanda kukeso na aura kice yabaki ga kayan lefe ba, me yayi zafi hakane ko kin gaji danine."

     Karasa maganar tayi cikin kuka da shashekar kuka.
       Jikin hajiya rabi ne yayi sanyi anan ta lura da rashin hankalin da tabuga take tashiga bawa yartata hakuri da bazata sake ba,

         ~~~~
A can abuja ummy na zaune ita da Nanna da Udaina autarsu sadauki, suna hira Hajiya rabi takirata, tana jin lokacin da Surayya takwace wayar har takashe, itama kiran sadauki tayi tace.
"Maza ka tura kuɗin kayan lefe wa Adama ta haɗa akai gidan Babanka, sai kayan faɗar kishiya nasan tana da kayanta kabata kuɗi zatayi wani hidimar dashi."

      "Toh ummy duk yanda kika ce."

        Zuciyarta cike da farin ciki tace.
"Allah yayi muku albarka ya bada zuria ɗayyiba."   
     "Amin" yace.



          Transfeer yayiwa Aunty Adama akan tayi sayayyan kayan lefe.
   Ai kuwa dake suma yan taya ɓera ɓari ne suka haɗa kan kata'i wanda sai da ya kusan haukatar da hajiya rabi gori kuwa da habaici.


       ...............
Cikin wata na biyu aka fara hidimar biki, a satin farkon sabon wata aka ɗaura auren Sadauki da Surayyan, dakyar ango yazo family nd Friend dan sai da ummy tayi masa faɗa, sannan yazo, kaman yanda hajiya rabi taso ayi haka kuwa bikin ya kasance sabida surayya itace yarta mace a cikin maza biyar shi yasa take masifar ji da ita,


        Abuja itama aka kawota, abin dariya hajiya rabi har da ita a masu kawo amarya, dake Nanna tabar gidan  a tsohon side ɗin sadauki aka saukesu, sai da dare suka haɗu da Nanna awjen dinner, kome yayi kyau haka suka gama hidimarsu aka taso, ya dawo gida shi da Nanna, dake shi yaja motar yana kallon yanda ya, kwantar da kanta tana barci har suka isa gida ya kashe motar yana kallon yanda take barci tsintar kanshi yayi da kyaleta tayi barcinta a gurin har sai ta farka shima gyara kujeran yayi yanda zai ji daɗin kwanciya, cikin.hikima ya kwantar da nata, aikuwa ta gyara kwanciyarta sosai,


             Suna fuskartan juna, numfashinsu yana haɗewa da juna, a hankali yake kallon fuskarta a dan hasken da yake kewaye da gidan zuba mata idanun yayi cikin nutsuwa bai taɓa kare mata kallo ba, sai yau bai taɓa lura da tarin baiwar da Allah yayiwa matarsa ba sai yau, haka kawai yaji wani abu me wuyar fassaruwa yana, yawo a cikin jininshi da kwakwalwarsa, yau ɗaya, amadadin ya cigaba da kallonta sai ya mike yafita yaje ya buɗe gidan sannan yazo ya ɗauketa a hankali buɗe ido tayi, a razane ganin shine tayi maza tarufe idanunta, wani shu'umin murmushi yasake, sannan ya cigaba da tafiya da ita, suna isa falo tazamo a hannunshi, tsuke fuska yayi sannan yace.
"Daman dan ki wahalar dani kikayi kamar kina bacci na ɗaukeki." yanda yayi maganar babu wasa yasa ta sunkuyar da kanta, tana wasa da pose ɗinta.

        Cire babban rigarshi yayi ya zauna, sam ya rasa gane irinta haushin kanshi yaji, can ya ɗago yace mata.
"Ina son tea me zafi."


            "Toh." tace a takaice, sannan ta juya tabar falon bin bayanta yayi da kallo yanda take tafiya kau da kanshi yayi cikin sauri yana me jin faɗuwar gaba haka kawai, har ta ɗauki kusan mintina goma sha, sannan tazo masa da flast na ruwan zafi da kayan haɗi ta ajiye zata bar falon, yana danna wayarshi, yace.

             Komawa tayi ta zauna cigaba da haɗa masa, tana gamawa ta mike har ta isa bakin kofarta yace.
"Baki ji ba, idan na gama ni zan kwashe kayan ko ihun kiranki zanyi ki cikin wannan tsohon dare."

        Dafe goshinta tayi kaman ta fasa ihu tace.
"Yau kuma ɗan lukuti, ya tsiro da sabon salo." haka ta koma har tana tankaɗi murmushi yayi ya cigaba da shan tea ɗinsa a hankali, gyara zama tayi a kujeran tacigaba da baccinta hankali kwnce, sai da yabar tayi nisa, ya buga table ɗin dake gabansu yace.
"Me kike nufi ni zanyi gadinki."

        Zama tayi tacigaba da gyangyaɗi har ya gama ya mike ya kai kayan kicin sannan ya dawo ya buga kujeran a firgice ta farka rike da kanta yana barazanar fashewa sabida ciwon kai, hawayene ya shiga sauka daga idanunta,a hankali sai ya tsinci kanshi da tausayinta, a kasan makoshinsa yace.
"Kiyi hakuri bansan zaki razana ba, koma ki kwanta zan tayaki kwanciya a falon."

          Girgiza kanta tayi, tace.
"A'a barshi zan koma ɗaki kawai,." a sanyaye tayi  maganar,


          Tafiya tayi tabarshi a gurin yana mamakin sabon yanayin da ya fada, a cikin daren yau.

       Dakyar ya koma ɗakinshi, ya shiga banɗaki ya faɗa wanka baya cire kayanshi, yana cire kaya amma ita take masa gizo nan ma dai sai da yayi dagaske ya fito bayan yayi alola, sallar nafilla yayi sannan ya kwanta bayan yayi addu'a.

Washi gari.....

Buɗan kai akayi da waleema, bayan angama aka kai amarya gidanta, inda masu halin cikin an kai amarya suka haɗu akayi musu nasiha, da su zauna lafiya. Sannan mutane suka watse. Sai da taga Mamuh zata bar gidan ta fara kuka irin na gulman nan....

     Dakyar aka rabasu da mamuh dan sun manne ana kuka.



         Bayan tafiyarsune, ta zauna shiru, har time ɗin Sallah, sannan ta mike tayi gabarta.


       10:30 na dare suka shigo shi da Adam, nan ya tarasu yayi musu nasiha, tare da taikamon adam, bayan tafiyar Adam ne shima ya rufe gidan ya wucce ɗakinshi.


         Tun surayya nasa ran zuwan ango har sarkin sata yayi gaba da ita. 

    Washi gari....
Kusan tare suka tashi agidan dan sun gaji, nanna da surayya suka kintsa gidan fess, sannan suka shiga kicin sukayi abinda zasu saka abakinsu. fitowa yayi sanye da sky blue boyel, agogonshi fari wacce ta dace da shigarsa, kome nashi yayi, kamshin turareshi ya sanar dasu yana falo surayya ce ta leko, daga inda ake cin abinci sukayi ido biyu tura masa baki tayi tare da murguɗawa, cikin shagwaɓa tafito, dan lokacin rigar baccintane, a jikinta itama nanna haka amma ita sanya himar dinta asamar rigar, itama surayya da himar ɗin tafito zafin wuta yasa tacire, dan rigar shara sharace.

           Sau ɗaya ya kalleta ya kauda kanshi dan bai dai cikar halitta ba, dan zan iya cewa tafi, nanna cika sosai dan tana da yar jiki, Nanna kuwa, bata da kiba amma a dire take ful daga sama harkasa chakwai 👌 take.

        Tana zuwa inda yake, ta zauna a kusa dashi, ɗagowa yayi zai mata magana ta kai masa light kiss,😱 ai bai san lokacin da yasake baki ba, fari tayi masa da ido, sannan ta zuɓe a jikinshi tace.
"Ina fushi da kai, akan me baka shigo ba jiya." goga masa na boons ɗinta take a kan cinyarshi, yana jin yanda suka fita ta samar rigarta, tsigar jikinshine yayi wani yarrr duk wani gashi na jikinshi sai da ya mike, ɗagota yayi, cikin kasalallaliyar murya yace.
"My sury ya isa haka, kiyi hakuri wani aiki nake cikin dare yasa na manta dake amma bazan sake ba."

      Mikewa tayi daga jikinshi tace.
"Ok tashi muje ka tayani zaɓen kayan da zan saka." 
     Sabida yanda jikinshi ya amshi abinda tayi masa bai san lokacin da ya mike ba, ya bu baƴanta suka shiga ɗakinta, suna shiga batayi abinda ya kaisu ba, tajuya ta fara niman, bakinshi cikin wani irin yanayi, so yake ya hanata amma ina, dan ita tayi alkawari duk kunyarta a wajwne daga ranar da tashiga gidan mijinta babu batun kunya.

        Tun sadauki yana kin biye mata har ya faɗa cikin tsundun, ta birkita shi, sosai sai dai koda yayi wani yunkuri sai yaji babu wani karfi ko kuzari (ai basai na faɗa muku kome)
          Gashi sury takai matsayin da take son kasancewa da mijinta duk da tsoron da ya cika mata ciki, dan san yau ce zata fara muamala da namiji........

*Page 24*
Duk yayi kane-kane a cikik ɗakin da ake bata kulawa yayi ciki ciki da fuska shi nan kar wani namiji ya taɓa mashi matarsa, dake shugaban asibitin ya faɗa musu abinda sadauki ya faɗa masa tun kawo Nanna asibitin duk sai suka watsa a ɗakin, har aka gama bata kulawa likitotin mata. suka fito daga ɗakin yana zaune dake sun tabbatar masa idan tafarka zasu iya tafiya, ciwon cikin period ne babu wata matsala, sai dai duk wata zasata ciwon ciki da zazzaɓi me zafi.

       Shiru dr Halima tayi tana kallonshi kafin ta ɗan kau da kanta gefe, tace.
"Sir matukar ana son ciwon ya daina damunta sai..."

"Sai me." ya tambayeta a kiɗime,
       Zare madubi idanunta tayi a nutse tace.
"Ka kwanta da hankalinka, matukar ana son ciwon ya daena sai mahaifarta ya ɗauki ciki, shine ciwo zai rabu da ita, amna akwai alura da maganin da zatana sha har natsawon kwanakin da tafara har ta gama."
       Gyaɗa kai yake kaman kaɗangare da yaji sanyin hunturu, miqa masa takardan tayi, karɓa yayi yace mata.
"Nagode sosai zan duba maganarki."

      Fita tayi daga ɗakin, zama yayi ya cigaba da kallon karin ruwan dake sauka a jikin hanunta, tsaki yayi bayan ya duba agogon hannunshi juya maganar dr halima yake tayi a ranshi can yace.
"Ina zansa kaina, a wannan yanayin idan su Ummy da Barde suka ji wannan labarin halin da nake ciki."  tallafe fuskarshi yayi cikin tashin hankali yake shafa kasumbarsa, har kusan karfe biu bata farka ba fita yayi yaje masjid yayi sallah yana dawowa yasamu har ta farka, abokan aikintane sukai ta mata tsiya da zolaya, da cewa.
"Eyye yar gatan miji kinga yanda ya ɗaukoki kuwa, duk ya firgice jikinshi sai rawa yake kar wani abu yasameki duk ya kori drs Mazan  akanki wai gaskiya mijinki yana sonki." murmushin yake tayi kawai, gyaran muryarshi sukaji daga bakin kofa, sake fuskarshi yayi ya kalleta yace.
"Zaki iya tashi dan yanzun zamu koma gida."
          Gyaɗa mashi kai tayi tana kallon kasa,
      "Ok taso muje."
        Gyara himar ɗinta tayi sukafita bayan tayiwa drs mata da maza godiya, abisa kulawar da suka bata.

"Wani mahaukachi ne yafaɗa miki ina sonki, akan me zaki faɗa musu ni ina sonki, kinga nayi kama da wanda...."

   "Ya isa haka waya faɗa maka nima ina sonkane wallahi ko a mafarki bana sonka balle a zahiri, kai har wani abin sone da za'asoka dubeka fa, wani lukuti dakai nima baka gabana." ta karshe maganar da murguɗa masa baki, cike da mamaki yake kallonta baki buɗe yce.
"Waye lukuti, ni abokin wasankine, lallai dan nayi hakuri na zauna dake shine zaki min rashin kunya ke har kin isa ki min rashin kunyamu, Allah yakaimu gida zanyi maganinki."


       Wani irin mahaukacin gudu yake, kaman zai turmushi mutane da suke gabanshi, suna isa gidan ana kiranshi a waya, kafin yayi wani yunkuri tafita da sauri tare da ɗaukar maganinta tashiga cikin gidan, ɗaukar wayar yayi yana kwafa ganin oganshine yasa yasake jan motarshi yafita daga gidan sai Ofishinsu na (NIA) , ana ganinshi ya dawo bakin aiki yasa kowa ya shi hankalinsa dan bayi da sauki a gurin aiki.


          Tattaunawa sukayi da manyanshi, har karfe biu na dare, sannan yabar ofishin tare da wasu jami'an masu tsaron lafiyanshi, yana shiga ciki ya nufi Bq dasu  har ɗakin da Yusra take, a kwance naɗe hannunshi yayi sannan ya, kallesu yace.
"Ku ɗauketa kukaita maiduguri kutabbarta da kun kaita har kofar gidansu ku ta faɗa musu tsintarta kukayi a hanya tana niman taimako, sannan ku shiryawa tafiya Cape town South African."

     "Yes sir." daukarta sukayi kamar wata tsima suka fita da ita suka jefata a bayan mota.

    Suna fita shima yafita zuwa cikin gidanshi.

Washi gari....
Juma'a ya fita da karfe shida, san masa waya akwai wasu kayan da aka shigo dasu daga can south african ta jirgi, dan haka airport suka dirka, dukkansu har da jami'an kula da safara da miyagun kwayoyi, suna saka har kusan 1pm babu kaya babu dalilinsu,

          Har sunyi niyyar barin wajen sai yaga wani mutum da wani abin rufe fuska da bai damu da mutumin ba, sai da yaga bayan hannunshi da shaidar Tattos anyi zanen kan kwarangwal da, kashin rufe idanunshi yayi yana nazarin inda ya taɓa ganin wannan abin, buɗe idanunshi yayi yabi mutumin da gudu shima jin ana binshi da gudu, yasa yasakai, can da yaga zasu rufa masa, ya tsaya cak, ya ɗauki wata yarinya daga cinyar Mamanta sanyawa yarinyar wuka yayi a wuya, ya juya ya kalle sadauki cikin murmushi, yace.

"Ahhhha Sadauki kake ashe baka mutu ba, ok kasa yaranka subani hanya na wucce idan ba haka ba zan kasheta."

        Kallon uwar yarinyar yayi wacce tashiga tashin hankali, sannan ya juya ya kalle yaranshi yace.
"Ku matsa ku kyaleshi,"  sosa giranshi yake yana magana, murmushi, mutumin yayi sannan yace.
"Nasan kayi hakane kawai dan ka, raina min hankali, kace yaranka suyi droping gun ɗinsu inba haka ba zan kasheta, uwartace da asara, ki faɗa masa yabarni na fice inba haka ba, zan kashe yarki ban damu ba."

      Cikin kuka tace.
"Don Allah karka masa kome, ka barmin yarinyata shekarata goma sha biyu da aure ban same haihuwa ba sai yanzun, don Allah karka bari na rasata she only my Child that i have plss don't let him kill my dota."

  Karɓan tayi tana share hawayen da ke zuba daga idanunta, tace.
"Gift"
     Shafa kanta yayi sannan yace.
"Am sorry my baby." lafewa tayia jikin uwarta.

A can bauchi kuwa hajiya rabi tashe mutanen gida da habaici da barka magana, yarta tayi goshi, yarinya tayi farin jini, badan Surayya tana taka mata birki ba, da ba karamin yaki hajiya rabi zata haddasa ba, a cikin gidansu😂 

      dan har Mamuh sai da aka mata gori lokacin da Aliyu yaji labari haushin surayya ya kamashi har kofar gidansu yazo sukayi rigima, Dakyar Mamuh da Capt Majid suka shiga rigimar dan Aliyu yace auren kwaɗayi zatayi, ita kuma tace auren biyayya, abinda yakai har tace masa bata ga amfanin soyayya dashi ba, tunda bai yarda kaddara ba, Chaiiii abin ya kazanta dan sai da ya tsika mata mari guda 2😂   da kuka tashiga cikin gida Mamuh ta shigo da niyyar bata hakuri, hajiya rabi ta tareta tare da cewa.

A cikin sati uku sadauki bai samu lokacin kanshi ba, karshe Adam ne ya ɗauki Nanna ya kaita gidansu dan baya zaman gida, ana shirin bikinshi shi yana can yana farautar miyagun mutane, 

     

     Lokacin da Adam yakaita gurin Ummy, suna da dare ummy ta kalleta tace.
"Safeenat mijinki zaiyi aure ya faɗa miki kuwa."

     Wani irin faɗuwar gaba taji wnda bata san dalilin haka ba, murmushi tayi kafin tace.
"Ya faɗa min tun wanca satin."

        Mamakine ya rufe ummy yanda Nanna ta iya gyata mata karya, kuma sadauki ya tabbarta mata bai faɗa mata ba, dan takare shi tace ya faɗa mata ɓata rai ummy tayi kafin tace mata.

"Ke baki kishin mijinkine, da bazaki damu ba,"

 Dariya sosai wanda tasan kawai tayine dan kar Ummy ta zargi wani abu tace.
"Ummy idan nayi kishin zai hanashi yin aurene, ba zai hanashi ba sai ma kwarin gwiwar da zan kara masa da borina, kuma ni atunanina kowace da zamanta zatayi ba akaina zata zauna ba, kyawun halinka shi zai wankeka, kuma ni anawa tunanin yanda ns ɗauki kishiya haka zata zo min idan na ɗauketa da zafi haka zata zo min idan kuma da sanyine haka zata zo min kenan ba bukatar ɗaga hankalin akan dan miji zaiyi aure Allah ya bashi ikon kaman adalci, kuma kwanciyar hankali baya wanzuwa a gida matukar babu zaman lafiya, shima mijin baya samun buɗe sai an same zaman lafiya Allah yasa mudace kawai Ummy."


       Al'ajabin da ban mamaki suks bayana a fuskar Ummy har Barde da shigowarshi kenan gidan jin abinda Nanna take faɗa yasa yaja ya tsaya yana mamakin yarinya karama da ita,
 amma Allah yabata baiwa da azanci hikima game da fikra, murmushi yayi sannan yace.
"Allah yayi miki albarka yajikan mahaifiyarki sannun y'ata."

    Nasiha suka mata sosai sannan Ummy tashige cikin part ɗin mijinta da taimaka masa.


   Shiru tayi tana nazarin irin zaman da suke da sadauki najin haushi juna wanda ko a zamanin da bata taɓa jin irin haka taya zata fara gyara tsakaninsu ita bata son wani yasan damuwarta......
