"Ya isa haka hajiya kinzo dubani ne ko kinzo kashe auren da kika tursasani akayi min, daga jiya zuwa yau naga tarin alkhairi a idanunta, sannan ban hango zafin kishin mijinta ba, ashe wannan ya isa na zauna da ita lafiya, da ni da Yaya sadauki tushenmu ɗaya, amma abisa yanda na lura ita tafini samun matsayi da kima a gurin iyayenshi, mama a yanzun ina jin ajikina banyiwa Aliyu adalci ba, kawai kiyi abinda ya kawoki ki tafi, don Allah tunda akwai waya zamuyi dake."

Hawayen da take son ɓoyewa tare da adana shi, sune sukayi nasaran saukowa daga cikin ramin idanunta, suka sauko har kuncinta, kallon kallo sukewa junansu, kauda kanshi yayi sannan yace.

"Ba zan maka dole ka zauna dani ba amma zan iya cewa kafin ka zarta da hukunci kayi tunani da nazari, karkayi abinda zakayi danasani nan gaba, shawarace ba umarni bace." Tana gama faɗin haka tafita daga motar inda tasa ɗan hank ta goge hawayenta.

           Jan motar yayi zuciyarshi cunkushe da tunani kala kala, gidanshi ya koma yana shiga cikin me tsaron gater ya tawo da sauri ya mika masa key yace.
"Gashi inji madam karama ta tafi, shine tace na baka key ɗin gidan."


           Karɓa yayi ranshi nakara ɓaci dukar motaeshi yayi da hannunshi idanushi sunyi jajur ranshi nakara tafasa.

          Shiga motar yayi yabar gidan ofis ya tafi can ma bai taɓuka kome ba, har lokacin tashin nanna yayi yaje ya ɗaukota, tunda tafito ta hango  ajiyar zuciya ta sauke dan duk yinin yau tayishi cikin tashin hankalin da tagani a cikin idanunshi sam bata sami sukuni ba, sai da ganshi. Ahankali ta isa gurin da yayi parkin motarshi buɗewa tayi tashiga sannan ta zauna juyawa tayi ta kalleshi, shima ita yake kallo, a hankali tace.
"Ina yini." a takaice ta gaisheshi, murmushin yake yayi sannan yace.
"Tunda na aikeshi bai dawo ba,..." cikin salon tsokana ya faɗa mata.

          Kauda kanta tayi, dan tsintar kanta tayi da jin abin banbara kwai.
     

      Shima jan bakinshi yayi gum har suka isa gida, lura tayi da gidan kaman su kaɗaine babu surayya, yasa tunda tashiga tacire kayanta ta shige kichin dan sama musu abinda zasu ci, tana aiki amma galibin tunanin inda surayya tashiga take har ta gama, takai mashi ɗaki,



     ............... Bauchi
      Tana ganin sun fita tayi maza ta haɗa kayanta tafito rufe gidan tayi sannan mikawa me tsaron gidan key tafita da sauri dan har tana harɗewa, dakyar tasamu lif aka kaita tashan mota na cikin garin abuja, anan tasame motar bauchi ya kusan tashi biyan kuɗin tayi sannan ta zauna.

               Karfe takwas da rabi motar tabar tashar.

             Karfe huɗu na yamma suka iso garin bauchi, daga tashar mass ta ɗauki keke napep sai dutsen tanshi biyanshi kuɗinsa yayi sannan ta ɗauki jakar kayanta sai cikin gidansu.

         A tsakar gida tasame matan gidan da yaransu da gudu yaran gidan suka isa gareta, saura kaɗan su kada ita sannan suka amshi jakar suka kai mata side ɗinsu gaida matan babanta tayi sannan ta mike tashiga side ɗinsu.

      Amaryansu hajiya rabice, da kada baki cikin son yin gulma tace.
"Kaii jama'a yaushe akayi auren da zafara yaji naga duniya."

        Shiru Maman maryam tayi itake bin hajiya rabi dan ita ubangiji ya taimaka ilimin addini yayi mata kawanya sam bata ɗauki rayuwarta da tsanani ba, shi yasa da Maman Fahad tayi magana ko ɗago kai batayi ba tacigaba da aikinta.



        Shiga ɗakin hajiya rabi surayya tayi dai dai hajiya tafito daga banɗaki kenan taga surayya cike da tsoro ta zaro idanunta tace.
"Surayya kece me ya kawoki lafiya kika zo, me yasa bazaki kirani ba."


          Cikin halin ko in kula tace.
"Wallahi bazan zauna da shi ba, haka kawai a cuce ni, da raina da lafiyata, kawai kusan yanda zakuyi akashe auren dan kuw.."


      Wanketa da mari Hajiya rabi tayi ranta na mata zafi cikin hargowa da ɗaga murya tace.
"Ni zaki janyowa abin faɗa sokike asami abin min gori a cikin kishiyoyina, duk yaran gidan nan wacece tasame miji irin, naki dan rashin sanin ciwon kai zaki ce bazaki zauna ba, wllhi kaman kin zauna."



           Kuka tasaka tace.
"Wallahi bazan zauna ba, akame za'a min dole." Abinda ya fusata hajiya rabi kenan ta ɗauki kwalbar turarenta ta jefeta a gaban goshinta ai kuwa cikin tsautsayi ya fasa mata, goshinta......"

         Banɗaki yashiga ya wanke fuskarshi sannan yafito, ko zama baiyi ba yayi musu sallama dan idan ya zauna zuciyarshi zata cigaba da raunana,


  ...............
Duk a falo koda yayi sallama nanna ce, ta amsa masa sallamar tare da mikewa ta amshi jakarshi, cike da kulawa yace.
"Me yasa bakiji aiki ba."

       Cikin rashin sabo tayi shiru sai da yasake tambayarta a karo na biyu sunkuyar kanta tayi sannan ta tattaro iya abinda zata iya cewa.
"Ai mun tafi st..ri.ke.."

       Dakyar ta haɗasu ta faɗa dan sai take ganin kaman tafara zakewa da yawa, har ɗaki takai masa kayanshi.

Rakiya da ido surayya tayi musu a zuciyarta tana hango abubuwa da dama,a tattare dasu. wanda bakowa zai iya hango haka ba, sai wanda yajibanci haka.

            Tana ajiye jakar tayi maza zata fito, tsare kofar ɗakin yayi fuskarsa babu alamun wasa kau da fuskarsa yayi ga barin kallonta sannan ya motsar da laɓɓanshi yace.
"Ki haɗa min ruwan wanka, sannan idan da hali ina son abinci mara nauyi bakuna dangin taliya ba, nidai abinda zan iya ɗaukana a cikina."

       Gyaɗai masa kai tayi jikinta na rawa, har zata wucce shi riko hanunta yayi, tana bayanshi a hankali yace.
"Nagode da kulawar da nasumu jiya daga gareki...." yana gams fadin haka ya sau mata hanun tafita.

*Page 34*

Safiyar juma'a da wuri yafita ya barta aɗakin tana gyara koina, gurin aikinshi yaje yayi musu bayanin ba zai samu ba,daga nan Asibitin da take aiki yaje, ya faɗa musu, sannan ya biya ta gidanshi ya kwaso musu kayan sawan su.
Sannan ya dawo yasamesu a kicin suna ta aikin abin karyawansu.
Shiga yayi ciki ya fara naɗe hannun rigarshi, sannan ya kalle Ummy yace.
"Ummyna barka da asuba, dame zan tayaku."
Cike da farin ciki ta kalleshi sannan tace.
"Lafiya lau, ya jikinka"
Ɗauke wuta yayi yaki kula maganar, wajen nannah ya isa sai da ya saci kallon ummy yaga bata kallonsu, cikin sauri ya sumbaci wuyarta da kumatunt, kunya ce ta kamata sauri tayi ta zame jikinta daga gareshi tana kallon ummyn wacce ta juya musu baya tana wanke hannunta, amma fuskarta ɗauke da murmushi ganin yanda Yaronta yake iya baiyana, Shaukinsa a gabanta lallai babu abinda ya kaiso Tasirantuwa. Tana gama wanke hannunta taja Larai daga ɗakin girkin ai kuwa yana ganin haka ya koma ya rumgumeta ta baya yana magana kasa-kasa, juyota yayi yana kallon cikin idanunta me ɗauke da yalwatacciyar gashin ido, iska ya wura mata yace.

Karfe sha ɗaya na safe yayi musu a cikin garin bauchi, daga Sir abubakar tafawa balewa In'tnl airport, a nan suka same motar gidan gwona, suna jiransu gidan sarki suka fara zuwa suka gaisheshi nan ya samuwa su sadauki albarka, inda ya nemi alfarman in ba zai takura ba, Zai bashi Y'arshi Hajra,...Shiru barde yayi sannan yace.

Murmushi sarki yayi bayan ya maida idanunshi kan sadauki wanda bai maraba da batun sarkin ba, sannan ya kalle nanna wacce jikinta yake rawa, sabida tsananin damuwa, yace.
"Sadeeq kaida Iyalinka bakuyi maraba da batuna ba, kai ka ɓata rai ita kuma tsoron zama da kishiya yasa jikinta na rawa, kenan dukkanku baku son bakuwa a cikinku, ku kwantar da hankalinku tunda baku so, Allah yayi muku albarka da zuri'a ɗayyiba.,"

Ba kunya Sadauki ya amsa da amin...

     Daga kofar fada, Government house suka wucce, nan ma sai da mai girma gwona yayiwa sadauki tayi ba kunya yace.
"Yallaɓe a min afuwa wanca da wadatar dani"
  Yana gama faɗin haka yasaci kallon nanna wacce duk sai ta takura.

  Nan ma alkhairi me girma gwona yayi mata sannan suka nufo gidan baba ladan.
    Jikinsu A sanyayye amma banda barde, shi yana gaba, su kuma suna baya, riko hannunta yayi suka sarkafe juna, har cikin gidan, suka shiga.
A lokacin Miemi ta ga zuwansu sai da tashiga har cikin gidan taga Nanna ce da gsk ba wata ba, aikuwa a guje tasheka gidansu, hajiya fatu tana zaune ta buga uban takume ga, Sailuba, a gefe ita da lawiza suna zage zage, da juna, fauziya da Fa'iza, suna can gefe kaman mayu sun manne da juna suna magana kuskus, baka jin me suke cewa, sai D'anki zuwaira itama karakainai yarantane yake tashi sai zaginsu take,
Kofar Maman Anas kuwa sabon fanti aka masa,da alamun me kuɗin gero ya sheko aure kenan,
"Hajiya Fatu kinga kuwa Nanna yanda ta koma kaman an wanke ta a injin Wallahi sun zo daga Abuja,"

  tana gama faɗin haka tare da haki.
Zubur ta mike tsaye tace.
"Jar ubanca ke bana son ɗiban albarka yar iska, da gaske kike."

"Mitsw akan me zan miki karya da kin ajiye bakin kudurinki akanta dan ko gidan zatazo tare da mijinta zata zo, baki ga yanda suka shiga gidan ba, wai nikam Hajiya fatu me zakiyiwa Nanna bayan abin arzikin da mijinta yayi mana, nifa yanzun na daina hassada dan ko kayi takice ga wanda kayiwa, kuma duk abinda ya faru kune silar kun hanata farin ciki da walwala, gashi Allah ya ɗagata, sama ya nuna miki duk sharrinki zai koma kanki tunda gashi nan Allah ya nuna miki izayarki da abinda kika mata, kun kwace mata soyayyarta gashi nan Allah ya nuna miki iyakarki tun ba'a je can ba an sako miki yaranki duk biyu, kinga wayanca guda biu kinsan me suke aikatawa, toh ki bincikesu, fatan da kikawa Nanna na tabi maza gashi nan ita batayi ba. Ke naki yaran sunyi tunda wanca zaman da sukayi niman mafita suke na zubda cikin jikinta...."

Dariya Miemi tayi irin na takaici, sannan tacigaba da cewa.
"ina son nayi karatu kin dakushe  mana baiwar da Allah yayi mana, na hakura na zauna duk mijin da yazo gurina yau gobe da lalata zai zo min, ni kaɗai nayi kuka har da hawaye, kin zalunceta Allah ya maida kanmu Wallahi Hajiya matukar Nanna tashigo baki nemi yafiyarta ba har Abada bazaki taɓa farin ciki ba kaii Hajiys kinyi abubuwa da yawa wallahi baki ga kome ba, sai ranar da kika waye gari kowa ya juya miki baya."

     Fita tayi da gudu, cikin tashin hankali da ɗimowa ko gabanta bata gani, abin yazo ga rashin dace wata j5 ta kawo itace unguwar ta sauke me motar yana kokarin yin rivass kawai yayi ciki da ita, ya haɗata da wata mota faɗuwa tayi, yunkurin mikewa tayi, J5 ɗin tabi ta kanta. Wani razananne kara tayi wanda sai da ya karaɗe unguwar baki ɗaya abinda ya ɗago matasan unguwar suka iso a guje, aka shiga ɗagata, "Nanna....Nanna...Nann..." wani yaro ne ya shiga gidansu ya faɗa ɗan kwali a hannun hajiya fatu ta fito, ganinta da Miemi tayi yasa ta kauda kanta tace.
"Karku bari ta taɓa min gawata, ni Nanna nakeso ta min kome sai Babana da Yayuna maza karku kaini ɗakin Hajiya fatu...".

......Gidan Baba ladan.
Kuwa Yahuza ne ya shiga ya shaida musu abinda ke faru lokacin Baba ladan yana cin kaniyar Sadauki inda yace sai ya sake masa Safeenat, ita kuwa kuka take tana manne a jikinshi, jin abinda yahuza ys faɗa yasa ta mike da gudu, tafita, ganin ana kokarin saka Miemi a mota da sauri ta isa gurin lokacin mieme tana kiranta, rike hanun miemi tayi tace.
"Gani nan Yar uwata,"
A hankali Miemi tace.
"Nan...Nan...Ki....Yaf.."
A jiyar zuciya tayi sannan ta sake hannun Nanna. A hankali Nanna take ja da baya ji tayi ya riketa gam, ta juya ta rungumeshi tana sake ajiyar zuciya.....

"Maza shafesu a jikinki, ki sake jiki dani ni ba uwar miji bace ni Uwace a gurinki karki saka kunya tayi miki shamaki dani,dan abinda zanyiwa su Nazfi shi zan miki, karɓa duk da nasan mijinki babu abinda zai miki amma kina amfani da wannan turaren, yana da kyau, idan ba kwanciya ba karki saka kinji ko ni bani da damuwa, insha Allah idan yasame lafiya dole nakaiki wajen Hajiya falmata.


          Duk sai kunya ya hanata sukuni, banɗaki tashiga ta gyara jikinta tsaf tafi cikin riga da wando, masu kyau na barci himar ta ɗaura akai sannan tafita dan bata ga ummy ɗakin ba, a hankali take tafiya kaman mara gaskiya haka tayi ta tafi har ɗakinshi.

         Dakyar yasamu hannun yayi mata sauki, sai lokacin ta tuna da abinda tagani wai dan taganshi da Towel, shi kuma kamshin mix ɗin turaren jikinta yake yashaka, janyota yayi suka kwanta, dukda haka hanunta na bakinshi dan kaman Yasame Big bom, haka yake tsotsa,😂


           Zare hannunta tayi daga bakinshi ta cukwikwaƴe himarta dan wani iri taga idanunshi sun koma mata, kaman wanda yasha wani abu,

            Ja dabaya tafara, ya janyota sosai, abinda yakewa surayyah shi yayi mata, wanda kusan shima ya amfana da haka.😉

     Lokacin da surayyah suka bar gidan baba ladan tana zaune a cikin motar kallonta Aliyu yayi sannan ya maida idanunshi kan hanya yace.
"Ban fahimci kome, ba kina nufin baki da Iddar Sadeeq, toh akan me,"

       "sabida ina sonka shiyasa naki yarda dashi wa kai zan bawa kowa kaina ba sai kai, Ka gamsu." Jin jina kai yayi cikin gamsu da abinda ta faɗa, yace.

Sosai ya riketa a jikinshi kifa kanta tayi a jikinshi ta sake kuka, me matukar cin rai. Tana rungume a jikinshi ya shiga gidansu da ita, hankalinsu hajiya fatu da yaranta yayi dubu ya tashi ganin gawan Yarta a kwance a k'asa jini na fita a jikinta, ɗago kai tayi cike da ɓacin rai ta ce.
"Bakar munafuka maiya kinzo kin cinye min kurwa y'a annamimiya, tsinaniya wallahi kaman yanda Mimie ta mutu kema sai kin bita, tunda nake bata taɓa min rashin kunya ba sai akanki na rantse matukar ina numfashi sai naga bayanki."Wannan maganar da tayi ba karamin ɗaga hankalin Sadauki yayi ba, har zai juya da ita su fita suka jiyo muryan daga ɗakin Maman Nanna tana cewa.
